        const cameraBanner=root.querySelector('.cleaning-sections .photo-template-banner');
        if(cameraBanner&&!cameraBanner.previousElementSibling?.classList.contains('task-photo-help')){

        const notificationCard=[...root.querySelectorAll('.setting-card')].find(card=>card.querySelector('h3')?.textContent.trim()==='알림 설정');

        const replacements=new Map([
          ['데모 메모리 이미지','사진'],['현재 원장','현재 합계'],['객실별 원장','객실별 청소 내역'],['수익 원장','청소비 내역'],['새 청소비 내역','추가 청소비'],['스냅샷','기준'],['fixture','데모 기록'],['수행 회차','청소 작업'],['회차 종료','청소 종료'],['제출 버전','제출 내용'],['데모 지급 이력','지급 이력'],['데모 금액','금액'],['브라우저 세션','현재 기록']
        ]);
        const walker=document.createTreeWalker(root,NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT),nodes=[];while(walker.nextNode())nodes.push(walker.currentNode);
        nodes.forEach(node=>{if(node.parentElement?.closest('.demo-strip'))return;let value=node.nodeValue;for(const [from,to] of replacements)value=value.split(from).join(to);value=value.replace(/\b(?:ATT|SUB|REPORT|PAY|LEASE|work|target)-[A-Za-z0-9-]+\b/gi,'').replace(/\s*·\s*v\d+(?:\.\d+)?\b/gi,'').replace(/\s*·\s*데모(?:\s+(?:기록|지급 이력|금액))?/g,'').replace(/\(데모\)/g,'').replace(/\s{2,}/g,' ');node.nodeValue=value;});
      }

      function applyAdminCopyPolicy(root) {
        if(state.role==='maid'){applyMaidCopyPolicy(root);return;}
        if(state.role!=='admin')return;
        root.querySelectorAll('.work-history-person-head p').forEach(paragraph=>{paragraph.textContent=paragraph.textContent.replace(/^당시 이름 스냅샷\s*·\s*/,'').replace(/\s*·\s*제출 버전.*$/,'');});
        root.querySelectorAll('.pay-ledger-room p').forEach(paragraph=>{paragraph.textContent=paragraph.textContent.split(' · ').slice(0,3).join(' · ');});
        root.querySelectorAll('.audit-note,.scenario-coach,.mobile-section-title p,.quick-booking-boundary,.quick-booking-guide-copy,.quick-booking-summary small,.quick-grid-status span:last-child,.assignment-random-copy p,.random-rule,.maid-order-lane-head p,.assignment-panel-head p,.maid-order-unassigned p,.assignment-target-rule,.work-history-unit-note,.work-history-contract,.work-history-hero p,.tab-header p,.pay-week-toolbar p,.pay-ledger-reconcile,.template-page > .notice-info,.template-row-version,.template-hero-stat,.template-hero p,.template-evidence,.template-timeline').forEach(element=>element.remove());
        root.querySelectorAll('.cleaning-cost-foot').forEach(foot=>{const range=foot.querySelector('b');foot.replaceChildren(range||document.createTextNode(''));});
        const costCard=root.querySelector('.cleaning-cost-card');
        if(costCard)costCard.setAttribute('aria-label',`청소비 예상 지출. 오늘과 이번 주 금액을 확인하고 주급 정산으로 이동합니다.`);
        const assignmentIntro=root.querySelector('.assignment-intro p');if(assignmentIntro)assignmentIntro.textContent=assignmentIntro.textContent.split(' · ')[0];
        root.querySelectorAll('.assignment-normal,.assignment-table .cell-sub').forEach(element=>element.remove());
        root.querySelectorAll('.assignment-route-reference').forEach(element=>{if(element.textContent.includes('담당 선택 후'))element.remove();});

        root.querySelectorAll('.toggle-row span').forEach(span=>{if(['외부 송금을 시작하기 전입니다.','확정 지급액이 생기면 이 자리에서 기록합니다.'].includes(span.textContent.trim()))span.remove();});
        const quickCopy=root.querySelector('.quick-booking-hero-copy');

      function renderBottomNav(nav) {
        return `<nav class="bottom-nav" aria-label="모바일 주요 내비게이션">${nav.map(n=>`<button type="button" data-action="nav" data-view="${n.id}" ${currentView()===n.id&&!state.detail?'aria-current="page"':''}>${icon(n.icon)}<span>${n.mobileLabel||n.label}</span></button>`).join('')}</nav>`;
      }

      function renderMain() {
        if (!state.loggedIn) return renderLogin();
        if (state.detail) return renderDetail();
        if (state.role==='admin') {
          if (state.adminView==='today') return renderCheckoutInspectionQueueSummary()+renderAdminToday();
          if (state.adminView==='rooms') return renderRooms();
          if (state.adminView==='maids') return renderMaids();
          return renderAdminMore();
        }
        if (state.maidView==='schedule') return renderMaidSchedule();
        if (state.maidView==='my') return renderMaidMy();
        if (state.maidView==='done') return renderMaidDone();
        if (state.maidView==='pay') return renderMaidPay();
        return renderMaidMore();
      }

      function renderCoach() {
        if (state.role==='admin'||state.scenario===0) return '';
        const cfg=SCENARIOS[state.scenario];
        return `<aside class="scenario-coach"><span class="step">${state.scenario}</span><div><strong>${esc(cfg.title)}</strong><p>${esc(cfg.next)}</p></div>${button('시나리오 재설정','reset','outline')}</aside>`;
      }

      function renderNetworkNotice() {
        if (state.network==='online' && state.listMode!=='stale') return '';
        return `<div class="notice notice-danger">${icon('sync')}<div><strong>${state.network==='offline'?'오프라인 · 읽기 전용':'오래된 데이터 · 변경할 수 없음'}</strong><br>마지막 연결 09:48. 인터넷에 연결한 뒤 다시 시도하세요.</div>${button('다시 시도','retry-network','outline')}</div>`;
      }

      function applyAdminCopyPolicy(root) {
        if(state.role==='maid'){applyMaidCopyPolicy(root);return;}
        if(state.role!=='admin')return;
        root.querySelectorAll('.work-history-person-head p').forEach(paragraph=>{paragraph.textContent=paragraph.textContent.replace(/^당시 이름 스냅샷\s*·\s*/,'').replace(/\s*·\s*제출 버전.*$/,'');});
        root.querySelectorAll('.pay-ledger-room p').forEach(paragraph=>{paragraph.textContent=paragraph.textContent.split(' · ').slice(0,3).join(' · ');});
        root.querySelectorAll('.audit-note,.scenario-coach,.mobile-section-title p,.quick-booking-boundary,.quick-booking-guide-copy,.quick-booking-summary small,.quick-grid-status span:last-child,.assignment-random-copy p,.random-rule,.maid-order-lane-head p,.assignment-panel-head p,.maid-order-unassigned p,.assignment-target-rule,.work-history-unit-note,.work-history-contract,.work-history-hero p,.tab-header p,.pay-week-toolbar p,.pay-ledger-reconcile,.template-page > .notice-info,.template-row-version,.template-hero-stat,.template-hero p,.template-evidence,.template-timeline').forEach(element=>element.remove());
        root.querySelectorAll('.cleaning-cost-foot').forEach(foot=>{const range=foot.querySelector('b');foot.replaceChildren(range||document.createTextNode(''));});
        const costCard=root.querySelector('.cleaning-cost-card');
        if(costCard)costCard.setAttribute('aria-label',`청소비 예상 지출. 오늘과 이번 주 금액을 확인하고 주급 정산으로 이동합니다.`);
        const assignmentIntro=root.querySelector('.assignment-intro p');if(assignmentIntro)assignmentIntro.textContent=assignmentIntro.textContent.split(' · ')[0];
        root.querySelectorAll('.assignment-normal,.assignment-table .cell-sub').forEach(element=>element.remove());
        root.querySelectorAll('.assignment-route-reference').forEach(element=>{if(element.textContent.includes('담당 선택 후'))element.remove();});
        const randomKicker=root.querySelector('.random-kicker');if(randomKicker)randomKicker.textContent='미배정 객실 자동 분배';
        const randomLive=root.querySelector('.random-live');if(randomLive)randomLive.textContent=state.randomAssignmentSnapshot?`${state.randomAssignmentSummary?.assigned||0}건 배정됨 · 아래에서 수정하세요`:'미배정 객실만 배정합니다.';
        const filterContext=root.querySelector('.assignment-filter-context span');if(filterContext)filterContext.innerHTML=`<strong>${esc(ROOM_TYPES[state.assignmentTypeFilter]?.name||'전체 객실 타입')}</strong> · 배정된 객실만 저장·통보`;
        root.querySelectorAll('.template-group-head p').forEach(paragraph=>{paragraph.textContent=`${paragraph.textContent.split(' · ')[0]} · 기본 청소요금`;});
        root.querySelectorAll('.template-group-head > .badge').forEach(badge=>badge.remove());
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-action="admin-pay-detail"]').forEach(button=>{
          button.textContent=button.textContent.replace('산출 보기','상세 보기');
          button.setAttribute('aria-label',(button.getAttribute('aria-label')||'').replace(/현재 원장/g,'현재 합계').replace(/산출 근거 보기/g,'청소 내역 보기'));
        });
        root.querySelectorAll('.toggle-row span').forEach(span=>{if(['외부 송금을 시작하기 전입니다.','확정 지급액이 생기면 이 자리에서 기록합니다.'].includes(span.textContent.trim()))span.remove();});
        const quickCopy=root.querySelector('.quick-booking-hero-copy');
        if(quickCopy)quickCopy.innerHTML=`<span class="quick-booking-kicker">빠른 예약 등록</span><div class="help-title"><h2>간편 예약 · 8월 15일 기준</h2>${infoTip('quick-booking','간편 예약','빈 날짜를 클릭하거나 같은 객실 행에서 가로로 드래그하세요. 터치는 0.35초 길게 누른 뒤 드래그합니다. 한 번 선택한 범위는 예약 한 건으로 저장됩니다.')}</div>`;
        root.querySelectorAll('p').forEach(paragraph=>{if(!paragraph.closest('.notice')&&/서버 영속|fixture|스냅샷|불변 사용자 ID|PIN lease|식별값|이력 연결키|원장 비연결|예약 가능과 현재 입실 가능을 별도로 판단|사진 원본은 앱에 저장하지 않음/.test(paragraph.textContent))paragraph.remove();});
        root.querySelectorAll('.meta').forEach(meta=>{if(/데모|스냅샷|정본/.test(meta.textContent))meta.remove();});
        addAdminHelp(root,'총 청소요금을 먼저 맞추고 가까운 객실로 배정','random-assignment','랜덤 배정','근무 가능한 메이드의 총 청소요금이 비슷해지도록 먼저 나누고, 같은 엘리베이터와 가까운 호수를 다음 기준으로 사용합니다. 결과는 저장 전에 담당과 순서를 바꿀 수 있습니다.',{rename:'랜덤 배정'});
        addAdminHelp(root,'메이드별 청소 순서 수정','assignment-result','청소 순서 수정','얼리 체크인·레이트 체크아웃의 예정 시각을 먼저 확인하고, 객실별 담당 메이드를 바꾸거나 위·아래 버튼으로 1번부터 순서를 정하세요. 담당과 순서가 정해진 객실만 저장·통보됩니다.');
        addAdminHelp(root,'내일 청소 배정','next-day-assignment','내일 청소 배정','근무 가능일을 제출한 메이드에게만 배정할 수 있습니다. 랜덤 배정 뒤에도 객실별 담당과 메이드별 청소 순서를 직접 바꾼 다음 저장·통보하세요.');
        addAdminHelp(root,'주간 근무 기록','work-history','주간 근무 기록','명은 해당 기록이 있는 메이드 수이고, 일은 메이드별 기록 날짜를 합한 값입니다. 가능 제출·담당 통보·실근무 완료를 서로 다른 표시로 확인합니다.');

        addAdminHelp(root,'현재 투숙 상태','occupancy','현재 투숙 상태','투숙 중에는 지금 체크아웃하거나 투숙 중 청소를 요청할 수 있습니다. 지금 체크아웃하면 공실·청소 필요 상태로 바뀝니다.');
        addAdminHelp(root,'예약·입퇴실','reservation-times','예약·입퇴실','한 고객의 체크인부터 체크아웃 순서로 입력합니다. 체크인 16:00보다 빠르거나 체크아웃 11:00보다 늦으면 자동으로 표시되고, 체크아웃 날짜에 퇴실 청소가 추가됩니다.');

        root.querySelectorAll('h3').forEach((heading,index)=>{if(/^왜 .+인가요\?$/.test(heading.textContent.trim()))addAdminHelpToElement(heading,`pay-detail-${index}`,'주급 계산','확정된 객실별 청소요금을 합산합니다. 폭탄방은 해당 객실만 기본요금을 한 번 더하고, 검수 대기 금액은 확정 주급에 포함하지 않습니다.');});

          const text=heading.textContent.trim();
          if(text==='계산 규칙'||text==='객실 사건 타임라인')heading.closest('.card')?.remove();
          if(text==='폭탄방 요금 원장')heading.textContent='폭탄방 청소비';
          if(text==='저장된 전체 과거 주차')heading.textContent='과거 주차';
        });
        root.querySelectorAll('th').forEach(heading=>{if(heading.textContent.trim()==='청소 정보·등록 근거')heading.textContent='청소 유형';});
        root.querySelectorAll('#modal-desc').forEach(description=>{
          if(/이력 연결키|식별값/.test(description.textContent))description.textContent='객실 타입과 엘리베이터를 수정합니다.';
          if(/시트 정본|예상시간만 데모/.test(description.textContent))description.textContent='객실 타입별 청소요금과 예상시간을 확인합니다.';
        });
        root.querySelectorAll('.field small').forEach(note=>{if(/PIN·청소·주급 이력|식별값/.test(note.textContent))note.remove();});
        root.querySelectorAll('.field label').forEach(label=>{if(/예약 식별|예약 ID/.test(label.textContent))label.closest('.field')?.remove();});

          const label=item.querySelector('span'),value=item.querySelector('strong');if(!label||!value)return;
          if(/PIN lease 영향|lease 영향/.test(label.textContent)){label.textContent='PIN 조회 처리';value.textContent='기존 조회 종료 후 다시 확인';}
          if(/이전 PIN lease/.test(label.textContent)){label.textContent='이전 PIN 조회';value.textContent='종료됨';}
          if(/활성 PIN lease/.test(label.textContent)){label.textContent='활성 PIN 조회';value.textContent=value.textContent.replace(/^[^·]+/,'1건');}
        });
        root.querySelectorAll('.choice').forEach(choice=>{
          const title=choice.querySelector('strong'),copy=title?.nextElementSibling;if(!title||!copy)return;
          if(title.textContent.includes('작업 재계획 완료')){title.textContent='청소 일정 변경 완료';copy.textContent=copy.textContent.replace('기존 수행 회차','진행 중 청소').replace('새 회차','새 청소');}
          if(title.textContent.includes('필요 PIN 교체')){title.textContent='도어락 PIN 확인 완료';copy.textContent='기존 PIN 조회를 끝내고 필요하면 PIN을 변경합니다.';}
        });
        root.querySelectorAll('.notice-info').forEach(notice=>{
          if(/새 작업은 시트 청소요금을 스냅샷|기존 확정 이력은 당시 기록|새 담당 구간이 타임라인/.test(notice.textContent))notice.textContent=/담당/.test(notice.textContent)?'저장하면 담당 메이드에게 통보됩니다.':'변경한 청소요금은 새 작업부터 적용됩니다.';
          if(/새 타입·엘리베이터는 이후 생성하는 작업/.test(notice.textContent))notice.textContent='변경한 정보는 새 작업부터 적용됩니다.';
        });
        const catalogCopy=root.querySelector('.catalog-summary-copy');
        if(catalogCopy){const occupied=ROOMS.filter(room=>room.occupancy==='occupied').length,vacant=ROOMS.filter(room=>room.occupancy==='vacant'&&!roomIsOnHold(room.no)).length,hold=ROOMS.filter(room=>roomIsOnHold(room.no)).length;catalogCopy.querySelector('span').textContent=`투숙 중 ${occupied}개 · 공실 ${vacant}개 · 확인 필요 ${hold}개`;catalogCopy.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',infoTip('room-status','객실 상태','고객 배정 가능은 공실 중 청소·촛불·운영 조건까지 모두 준비된 객실에만 표시됩니다.'));}
        const replacements=new Map([
          ['객실별 청소 원장 합계','청소 내역 합계'],['객실별 청소 원장','객실별 청소 내역'],['주급 산출 원장','주급 청소 내역'],['현재 원장 예상','승인 시 예상'],['현재 원장','현재 합계'],['공통 원장 산식','청소 내역 합계'],['카드·예약표 공통 원장','예약 일정'],['다중 예약 원장','예약 일정'],['비공개 퇴실 청소 초안','퇴실 청소 미배정'],['잠근 지급 기록액','지급 기록액'],['잠근 지급액','지급 기록액'],['지급 진행 스냅샷','지급 진행 상태'],['원장 변동 · 정정 필요','금액 확인 필요'],['지급 대기(OPEN)','지급 대기'],['지급 진행(PAYING)','지급 진행'],['정산 확인 필요(CHECK)','정산 확인 필요'],['지급 완료(PAID)','지급 완료'],['송금하지 않음 · OPEN 복귀','송금하지 않음 · 지급 대기'],['OPEN 복귀','지급 대기 복귀'],['잠근 수익 ID','청소 내역'],['수익 ID','청소 내역'],['잠근 수익','청소 내역'],['포함 수익','청소 내역'],['미지급 수익','미지급 청소비'],['PIN lease','PIN 조회 기록'],['활성 lease','활성 PIN 조회'],['lease 영향','PIN 조회 영향'],['lease','PIN 조회'],['기존 수행 회차','기존 청소 작업'],['수행 회차','청소 작업'],['기존 회차','기존 청소 작업'],['기존 청소 작업는','기존 청소 작업은'],['새 회차 생성','새 청소 생성'],['중단 이력으로 보존','중단 처리하고'],['상태 재계산','상태 다시 확인'],['재계산 완료','확인 완료'],['재검증','다시 확인'],['충돌 종결','충돌 조치 완료'],['충돌 조치 완료·상태 다시 확인','조치 완료'],['종결 전','조치 완료 전'],['종결 시각','조치 시각'],['기존 사건 삭제 금지','현재 상태 변경 전 확인'],['체크아웃 투숙 상태 복원을 추가합니다.','체크아웃 시각까지 투숙 중으로 표시합니다.'],['투숙 상태 복원와','투숙 상태 복원과'],['점유 재개 보정','투숙 상태 복원'],['점유 보정','투숙 상태'],['점유 재개','투숙 상태 복원'],['보정 대기','변경 대기'],['보정 완료','변경 완료'],['보정 이벤트','변경 기록'],[' · 원문 미표시',''],['브라우저 시뮬레이션','데모 화면'],['정적 파일','데모 화면'],['fixture','데모 기록'],['fingerprint','확인값'],['예약 ID별','예약별'],['예약 ID','예약'],['예약 식별','예약'],['식별값','확인값'],['식별자','확인값'],['스냅샷','기준'],['수익 원장','청소 내역'],['폭탄방 요금 원장','폭탄방 청소비'],['산출 원장','청소 내역'],['원장','내역'],['시트 정본','기본 요금'],[' · 8월 시트',''],['제출 당시 기준','제출 당시 기준'],['제출 버전 고정','제출 내용'],['현재 활성 템플릿 상세','템플릿 상세'],[' · 상태 이력 보존',''],['메이드 지정 · 사유 입력 · 삭제/복구 이력','메이드별 기록 관리'],['PIN 원문을 제외한 예약·업무·지급 감사 이력','예약·업무·지급 변경 내역'],['시트 청소요금·데모 예상시간과 템플릿','청소요금과 사진 템플릿'],['로그인 비밀번호는 객실 PIN과 분리','계정과 로그인 설정'],['OPEN','지급 대기'],['PAYING','지급 진행'],['CHECK','정산 확인 필요'],['PAID','지급 완료']
        ]);
        const walker=document.createTreeWalker(root,NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);const nodes=[];while(walker.nextNode())nodes.push(walker.currentNode);
        nodes.forEach(node=>{if(node.parentElement?.closest('.demo-strip'))return;let value=node.nodeValue;for(const [from,to] of replacements)value=value.split(from).join(to);value=value.replace(/\bLEASE-[A-Za-z0-9-]+\b/g,'기존 PIN 조회').replace(/\s*·\s*데모/g,'').replace(/\(데모\)/g,'').replace(/투숙 상태 복원와/g,'투숙 상태 복원과').replace(/중단 처리하고하고/g,'중단 처리하고').replace(/기존 청소 작업는/g,'기존 청소 작업은').replace(/청소 작업를/g,'청소 작업을').replace(/데모 데모 기록/g,'데모 기록');node.nodeValue=value.trim()==='데모'?'':value;});
        root.querySelectorAll('.info-item').forEach(item=>{const label=item.querySelector('span'),value=item.querySelector('strong');if(label?.textContent.trim()==='PIN 조회'&&value?.textContent.includes('기존 조회 종료'))label.textContent='PIN 조회 처리';});
        root.querySelectorAll('.badge').forEach(badge=>{if(!badge.textContent.trim())badge.remove();});
      }

      function renderTopbar() {
        return `<header class="topbar">
          <div class="topbar-title"><h1>${esc(titleForView())}</h1><p>한국시간 · 마지막 동기화 2026.08.14 ${state.network==='online'?state.time:'09:48'} ${state.network==='online'?'':'· 읽기 전용'}</p></div>
          <div class="topbar-actions">
            <button class="icon-btn" type="button" data-action="alerts" aria-label="알림함 열기">${icon('bell')}<span class="count-dot">${state.role==='admin'?6:3}</span></button>
            <button class="btn btn-outline" type="button" data-action="switch-role" aria-label="${state.role==='admin'?'메이드 보기':'관리자 보기'}">${icon('users','icon-sm')}<span>${state.role==='admin'?'메이드 보기':'관리자 보기'}</span></button>
          </div>
        </header>`;
      }

      function renderBottomNav(nav) {
        return `<nav class="bottom-nav" aria-label="모바일 주요 내비게이션">${nav.map(n=>`<button type="button" data-action="nav" data-view="${n.id}" ${currentView()===n.id&&!state.detail?'aria-current="page"':''}>${icon(n.icon)}<span>${n.mobileLabel||n.label}</span></button>`).join('')}</nav>`;
      }

      function renderMain() {
        if (!state.loggedIn) return renderLogin();
        if (state.detail) return renderDetail();
        if (state.role==='admin') {
          if (state.adminView==='today') return renderCheckoutInspectionQueueSummary()+renderAdminToday();
          if (state.adminView==='rooms') return renderRooms();
          if (state.adminView==='maids') return renderMaids();
          return renderAdminMore();
        }
        if (state.maidView==='schedule') return renderMaidSchedule();
        if (state.maidView==='my') return renderMaidMy();
        if (state.maidView==='done') return renderMaidDone();
        if (state.maidView==='pay') return renderMaidPay();
        return renderMaidMore();
      }

      function renderCoach() {
        if (state.role==='admin'||state.scenario===0) return '';
        const cfg=SCENARIOS[state.scenario];
        return `<aside class="scenario-coach"><span class="step">${state.scenario}</span><div><strong>${esc(cfg.title)}</strong><p>${esc(cfg.next)}</p></div>${button('시나리오 재설정','reset','outline')}</aside>`;
      }

      function renderNetworkNotice() {
        if (state.network==='online' && state.listMode!=='stale') return '';
        return `<div class="notice notice-danger">${icon('sync')}<div><strong>${state.network==='offline'?'오프라인 · 읽기 전용':'오래된 데이터 · 변경할 수 없음'}</strong><br>마지막 연결 09:48. 인터넷에 연결한 뒤 다시 시도하세요.</div>${button('다시 시도','retry-network','outline')}</div>`;
      }

      function renderListState(content, kind='queue') {
        if (state.listMode==='loading') return `<section class="card" aria-busy="true" aria-label="불러오는 중"><div class="skeleton-list"><div class="skeleton"></div><div class="skeleton"></div><div class="skeleton"></div></div></section>`;
        if (state.listMode==='empty') return `<section class="card state-panel"><div class="state-icon">${icon('check','icon-lg')}</div><h2>표시할 항목이 없습니다</h2><p>현재 선택한 날짜에 실제 운영 항목이 0건입니다.</p></section>`;
        if (state.listMode==='filterEmpty') return `<section class="card state-panel"><di

===== 'task-photo' occurrence 4 line 6153 =====

udit-note" style="margin:10px 0">예약·청소·충돌 이력은 삭제하지 않고 운영 재개 뒤에도 감사 이력에 보존됩니다.</p>${button('운영 재개','resume-operation','success',`data-id="${no}"`)}</section>`;
        }
        const conflictLocked=no==='332'&&state.conflict==='active';
        return `<section class="card card-pad"><div class="section-head"><h3>운영 상태</h3>${statusBadge(conflictLocked?'충돌 잠금':'정상',conflictLocked?'amber':'green')}</div><p class="audit-note">${conflictLocked?'출입 충돌 해결 전에도 별도 운영 중지는 가능하며 예약·작업 이력은 삭제하지 않습니다.':'모든 객실 상태에서 운영 중지할 수 있으며, 다른 객실 배정은 이 흐름 안에서만 진행합니다.'}</p>${button('운영 중지·대체 객실','operation-status','danger',`data-id="${no}"`)}</section>`;
      }

      function mergeRoomOperationPanel(html,no) {
        const replacement=renderRoomOperationDetailPanel(no);
        const startMarker='<section class="card card-pad"><div class="section-head"><h3>운영 상태</h3>', timelineMarker='</section><section class="card card-pad"><h3>객실 사건 타임라인';
        const start=html.lastIndexOf(startMarker), end=start<0?-1:html.indexOf(timelineMarker,start);
        if(start<0||end<0)return html;
        return html.slice(0,start)+replacement+html.slice(end+'</section>'.length);
      }
      function mergeRoomIssuesPanel(html,no) {
        const markers=['<section class="card card-pad"><div class="section-head"><h3>청소 작업</h3>','<section class="card card-pad"><div class="section-head"><h3>예약·청소 영향</h3>'];
        const indexes=markers.map(marker=>html.indexOf(marker)).filter(index=>index>=0),insertAt=indexes.length?Math.min(...indexes):-1;
        if(insertAt<0)return html;
        return html.slice(0,insertAt)+renderRoomIssuesSection(no)+html.slice(insertAt);
      }

      function renderCatalogRoomDetail(no) {
        const room=ROOMS.find(item=>item.no===no),type=ROOM_TYPES[room?.type||'standard'],hold=roomDataIssue(no);
        if(!room)return renderCoach()+detailHeader('객실을 찾을 수 없습니다','8월 객실 마스터를 다시 확인해 주세요.');
        return renderCoach()+renderNetworkNotice()+detailHeader(`${no}호`,`${type.name} · ${elevatorLabel(room)}`)+`<div class="detail-grid"><div class="detail-stack"><section class="card card-pad"><div class="section-head"><div><h3>정보 확인 필요</h3><p class="audit-note">현재 투숙 상태를 입력하면 일반 객실로 전환됩니다.</p></div>${statusBadge('확인 필요','amber')}</div><div class="notice notice-warning"><div><strong>${esc(hold)}</strong><br>관리자는 아래 객실 정보 수정에서 현재 상태를 직접 확정할 수 있습니다.</div></div></section></div><aside class="detail-stack"><section class="card card-pad"><h3>변경 전 제한</h3><p class="audit-note">확인 완료 전에는 예약·PIN·청소 배정만 제한합니다. 객실 기본정보 수정은 열려 있습니다.</p></section><section class="card card-pad"><h3>객실 사건 타임라인</h3>${renderTimeline(no)}</section></aside></div>`;
      }

      function renderRoomCleaningFallbackPanel(no) {
        return `<section class="card card-pad"><div class="section-head"><h3>청소 작업</h3>${statusBadge(statusLabel(state.jobs[no]),roomNeedsCleaningNow(no)?'amber':'neutral')}</div>${renderManualCleaningToggle(no)}</section>`;
      }
      function mergeRoomCleaningPanel(html,no) {
        if(html.includes(`data-room-cleaning-panel="${no}"`))return html;
        const marker='<div class="detail-stack">',index=html.indexOf(marker);
        return index<0?html:html.slice(0,index+marker.length)+renderRoomCleaningFallbackPanel(no)+html.slice(index+marker.length);
      }
      function renderRoomDetail(no) {
        const room=ROOMS.find(item=>item.no===no);
        if(roomIsOnHold(no))return mergeRoomBasicsPanel(mergeRoomCleaningPanel(renderCatalogRoomDetail(no),no),no);
        if(no==='332'&&['active','resolved'].includes(state.conflict)) {
          return mergeRoomBasicsPanel(mergeRoomCleaningPanel(mergeRoomIssuesPanel(mergeRoomOperationPanel(renderRoomConflict332(),no),no),no),no);
        }
        return mergeRoomBasicsPanel(mergeRoomCleaningPanel(mergeRoomIssuesPanel(mergeRoomOperationPanel(renderRoomDetailStandard(no),no),no),no),no);
      }

      function taskRequirements(no) {
        const task=taskState(no),snapshot=task.templateSnapshot||snapshotForAttempt(no,state.cleaningAttempts?.[task.attemptId]),requiredUploads=task.uploads.filter(upload=>upload.required),requiredDone=requiredUploads.every(upload=>upload.status==='done'),failed=task.uploads.some(upload=>upload.status==='failed'||upload.status==='uploading');
        return {task,snapshot,requiredDone,failed,requiredUploads};
      }
      function taskUploadByIdentity(no,photoId,attemptId) {
        const task=state.taskInputs?.[no];
        if(!attemptId||task?.attemptId!==attemptId)return null;
        const upload=task.uploads?.find(item=>item.id===photoId);
        return upload?{task,upload}:null;
      }
      function taskUploadForMutation(no,photoId,attemptId,allowedJobs=['cleaning']) {
        if(!maidCanEditCleaning(no,allowedJobs))return null;
        const resolved=taskUploadByIdentity(no,photoId,attemptId),currentId=currentAttemptId(no);
        return resolved&&currentId===attemptId?resolved:null;
      }
      function taskPhotoInputId(no,photoId,source) { return `task-photo-${source}-${no}-${photoId}`.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g,'-'); }
      function taskZoneGroups(task) {
        const groups=[],byZone=new Map();
        task.uploads.forEach(upload=>{
          const zone=upload.zone||'사진';
          if(!byZone.has(zone)){const group={zone,uploads:[]};byZone.set(zone,group);groups.push(group);}
          byZone.get(zone).uploads.push(upload);
        });
        return groups;
      }
      function taskUploadStatusText(upload) {
        if(upload.status==='uploading')return '사진 확인 중';
        if(upload.status==='failed')return '전송 실패 · 다시 촬영';
        if(upload.status==='done')return upload.image?.objectUrl?'현장 촬영 완료':'사진 완료';
        return upload.required?'필수 사진 없음':'선택 사진 없음';
      }
      function taskPhotoVisual(upload) {
        if(upload.image?.objectUrl)return `<img src="${esc(upload.image.src)}" alt="${esc(upload.label)} 현장 촬영 미리보기">`;
        if(['done','failed'].includes(upload.status)&&upload.image)return inspectionPhotoSvg(upload,upload.label);
        return icon(upload.status==='uploading'?'clock':'camera');
      }
      function taskZoneCardMarkup(no,group,task,editable) {
        const attemptId=task.attemptId||'',requiredUploads=group.uploads.filter(item=>item.required),photosDone=requiredUploads.every(upload=>upload.status==='done'),failed=group.uploads.some(upload=>upload.status==='failed'||upload.status==='uploading'),hasRequired=requiredUploads.length>0,complete=hasRequired&&photosDone&&!failed;
        const photos=`<div class="task-zone-photos">${group.uploads.map(upload=>{
          const cameraId=taskPhotoInputId(no,upload.id,'camera'),galleryId=taskPhotoInputId(no,upload.id,'gallery'),busy=upload.status==='uploading',captured=upload.status==='done'||upload.status==='failed'||!!upload.image?.objectUrl;
          return `<article class="task-zone-photo ${upload.status}" data-photo-slot="${esc(upload.id)}"><div class="task-zone-photo-main"><span class="task-zone-photo-visual">${taskPhotoVisual(upload)}</span><div class="task-zone-photo-copy"><strong>${esc(upload.label)} · ${upload.required?'필수':'선택'}</strong><span>${esc(upload.description||'청소 완료 상태를 촬영합니다.')}</span><span class="photo-state" aria-live="polite">${esc(taskUploadStatusText(upload))}${upload.instanceCount>1?` · ${upload.instance}/${upload.instanceCount}`:''}</span></div></div>${editable?`<div class="task-zone-photo-actions"><button class="btn btn-primary" type="button" data-action="capture-task-photo" data-room="${no}" data-photo="${esc(upload.id)}" data-attempt="${esc(attemptId)}" aria-label="${no}호 ${esc(group.zone)} ${esc(upload.label)} ${captured?'다시 촬영':'바로 촬영'}" ${busy?'disabled':''}>${icon('camera','icon-sm')}${captured?'다시 촬영':'바로 촬영'}</button><button class="btn btn-outline" type="button" data-action="choose-task-photo" data-room="${no}" data-photo="${esc(upload.id)}" data-attempt="${esc(attemptId)}" aria-label="${no}호 ${esc(group.zone)} ${esc(upload.label)} 갤러리에서 선택" ${busy?'disabled':''}>갤러리</button>${captured?`<button class="btn btn-outline icon-only" type="button" data-action="remove-task-photo" data-room="${no}" data-photo="${esc(upload.id)}" data-attempt="${esc(attemptId)}" aria-label="${no}호 ${esc(group.zone)} ${esc(upload.label)} 사진 삭제" ${busy?'disabled':''}>${icon('x','icon-sm')}</button>`:'<span aria-hidden="true"></span>'}</div><input id="${cameraId}" hidden type="file" accept="image/*" capture="environment" data-control="task-photo-file" data-source="camera" data-room="${no}" data-photo="${esc(upload.id)}" data-attempt="${esc(attemptId)}"><input id="${galleryId}" hidden type="file" accept="image/*" data-control="task-photo-file" data-source="gallery" data-room="${no}" data-photo="${esc(upload.id)}" data-attempt="${esc(attemptId)}">`:''}</article>`;
        }).join('')}</div>`;
        return `<section class="task-zone-card ${complete?'complete':''} ${failed?'failed':''}" aria-labelledby="task-zone-${no}-${groupsafe(group.zone)}"><div class="task-zone-head"><div><h4 id="task-zone-${no}-${groupsafe(group.zone)}">${esc(group.zone)}</h4><p>필수 ${requiredUploads.length}장 · 선택 ${group.uploads.length-requiredUploads.length}장</p></div>${statusBadge(complete?'구역 완료':failed?'사진 확인 필요':hasRequired?'촬영 필요':'선택',complete?'green':failed?'red':hasRequired?'amber':'neutral')}</div>${photos}</section>`;
      }
      function groupsafe(value) { return String(value||'zone').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9가-힣_-]/g,'-'); }
      function renderRoomIssueInput(no,editable) {
        const draft=roomIssueDraft(no),records=visibleRoomIssueRecords(no),hasDraft=!!draft.note.trim()||draft.photos.length>0;
        const types=['냄새','청소 미흡','시설 고장','비품 부족·파손','분실물','기타'];
        const editor=editable?`<div class="room-issue-form"><div class="notice notice-warning" style="margin:0"><div><strong>개인정보 입력 금지</strong><br>고객 이름·휴대폰 번호·이메일은 메모와 사진 파일명에 남기지 않습니다.</div></div><div class="room-issue-form-grid"><div class="field"><label for="room-issue-type-${no}">특이사항 유형 · 선택</label><select id="room-issue-type-${no}" class="select-control" data-control="room-issue-type" data-room="${no}"><option value="">유형 선택 안 함</option>${types.map(type=>`<option value="${type}" ${draft.type===type?'selected':''}>${type}</option>`).join('')}</select></div><div class="field"><label for="room-issue-note-${no}">업무 메모 · 선택</label><textarea id="room-issue-note-${no}" class="input-control task-note" rows="3" data-control="room-issue-note" data-room="${no}" placeholder="개인정보를 제외한 객실 상태를 적어 주세요.">${esc(draft.note)}</textarea></div></div><div class="room-issue-picker"><div class="room-issue-picker-copy"><strong>이미지 여러 장</strong><span>한 번에 여러 이미지를 선택하고 등록 전 개별 제거할 수 있습니다.</span></div><input id="room-issue-files-${no}" class="sr-only" type="file" accept="image/*" multiple tabindex="-1" data-control="room-issue-files" data-room="${no}"><button class="btn btn-outline" type="button" data-action="choose-room-issue-files" data-room="${no}">${icon('camera','icon-sm')}이미지 선택</button></div>${renderRoomIssueDraftPhotos(no)}<button class="btn btn-primary btn-block" type="button" data-action="save-room-issue" data-room="${no}" ${hasDraft?'':'disabled'}>특이사항 등록 · 선택 ${draft.photos.length}장</button><p class="audit-note" style="margin:0">현재 탭 메모리에서만 미리보기를 유지하는 데모이며 실제 서버 업로드·영속 저장이 아닙니다.</p></div>`:'<p class="audit-note" style="margin:12px 0 0">메이드는 청소 중에만 특이사항을 등록할 수 있습니다. 저장된 기록은 관리자에게 그대로 공유됩니다.</p>';
        return `${editor}${records.length?renderRoomIssueRecords(no):editable?'':'<div class="inline-empty"><h3>등록된 특이사항이 없습니다</h3><p>메이드가 저장한 특이사항과 사진이 여기에 표시됩니다.</p></div>'}`;
      }
      function renderCleaningInputSection(no,key,title,meta,body) {
        const titleId=`cleaning-section-${groupsafe(no)}-${groupsafe(key)}`;
        return `<section class="cleaning-section" data-cleaning-section="${esc(key)}" aria-labelledby="${titleId}"><div class="cleaning-section-head"><h3 id="${titleId}">${esc(title)}</h3><span data-cleaning-section-meta>${esc(meta)}</span></div><div class="cleaning-section-body">${body}</div></section>`;
      }
      function renderTaskInputSections(no,job) {
        const {task,snapshot,requiredUploads}=taskRequirements(no), occupied=ROOMS.find(r=>r.no===no)?.occupancy==='occupied';
        const editable=maidCanEditRoomIssue(no,job),bomb=bombRoomReport(no),bombMeta=bombRoomStatusMeta(bomb),groups=taskZoneGroups(task);
        const doneUploads=task.uploads.filter(upload=>upload.status==='done').length,type=ROOM_TYPES[snapshot?.typeId||ROOMS.find(room=>room.no===no)?.type||'standard'];
        const requiredGroups=groups.filter(group=>group.uploads.some(item=>item.required)),completedGroups=requiredGroups.filter(group=>group.uploads.filter(item=>item.required).every(item=>item.status==='done')).length;
        const tvSafety=snapshot?.photos?.some(item=>item.id==='tv-on'||String(item.id).startsWith('tv-on-'))?' TV는 켜고 계정·QR·알림 없는 기본 화면으로 촬영하세요.':'';
        const photoBody=`<div class="photo-template-banner"><div><strong>${esc(type.name)} · ${esc(snapshot?.name||'퇴실 청소')}</strong><span>필수 사진 ${requiredUploads.length}장 · 구역 버튼을 눌러 바로 촬영하세요.</span></div><div class="badge-row">${statusBadge(`${completedGroups}/${requiredGroups.length} 구역 완료`,completedGroups===requiredGroups.length?'green':'amber')}</div></div><div class="task-zone-progress"><div><span>완료 구역</span><strong>${completedGroups}/${requiredGroups.length}</strong></div><div><span>사진 촬영</span><strong>${doneUploads}/${task.uploads.length}</strong></div></div><div class="task-zone-safety">${icon('shield','icon-sm')}<span><strong>촬영 전 개인정보를 확인하세요.</strong><br>연락처·얼굴·고객 물품·거울 속 사람은 제외하세요.${tvSafety}</span></div><div class="task-zone-grid">${groups.map(group=>taskZoneCardMarkup(no,group,task,editable)).join('')}</div>`;
        const candleBody=`<p class="audit-note" style="margin:12px 0 0">청소 결과를 입력하는 동안만 객실의 최종 촛불 수량을 조정할 수 있습니다.</p><div class="candle-stepper" role="group" aria-label="${no}호 청소 결과 촛불 수량"><button class="btn btn-outline" type="button" data-action="task-candle-change" data-room="${no}" data-delta="-1" aria-label="촛불 1개 줄이기" ${!editable||task.candle<1?'disabled':''}>−</button><div class="candle-stepper-value" aria-live="polite"><strong>${task.candle||0}개</strong><span>청소 완료 후 객실 수량</span></div><button class="btn btn-outline" type="button" data-action="task-candle-change" data-room="${no}" data-delta="1" aria-label="촛불 1개 늘리기" ${!editable||task.candle>=9?'disabled':''}>+</button></div>`;
        return `<div class="cleaning-sections">${renderCleaningInputSection(no,'photos','구역별 사진 촬영',`${completedGroups}/${requiredGroups.length} 구역`,photoBody)}${renderCleaningInputSection(no,'bomb-room','폭탄방 신고',bombMeta.label,renderBombRoomPanel(no,job))}${renderCleaningInputSection(no,'room-issues','객실 특이사항',`${visibleRoomIssueRecords(no).length}건 · 선택 ${task.issueDraft.photos.length}장`,renderRoomIssueInput(no,editable))}${occupied?'':renderCleaningInputSection(no,'candles','촛불',`${task.candle||0}개`,candleBody)}</div>`;
      }
      function cleaningPrimary(no,job) {
        const req=taskRequirements(no),access=attemptAccessStatus(no);
        if(['scheduled','claimed','reclean'].includes(job)) return {label:access.allowed?(job==='reclean'?'재청소 시작':'일 시작'):access.reason,action:'start-cleaning',disabled:!access.allowed||isLocked()};
        if(job==='cleaning') { const photosLeft=req.requiredUploads.filter(item=>item.status!=='done').length;return {label:photosLeft?`남은 필수 사진 ${photosLeft}장`:'현장 완료',action:'field-complete-v2',disabled:!!photosLeft||req.failed||isLocked()}; }
        if(job==='upload'&&req.failed) return {label:'미전송 재시도',action:'retry-all-task-photos',disabled:isLocked()};
        if(job==='upload') return {label:'청소 전체 제출',action:'submit-cleaning-v2',disabled:!req.requiredDone||isLocked()};
        if(job==='inspection'||job==='approved') return {label:'제출 결과 보기',action:'demo-info',disabled:false};
        return {label:'상세 확인',action:'demo-info',disabled:false};
      }
      function maidCanStart(no) {
        const room=ROOMS.find(r=>r.no===no),job=state.jobs[no],task=taskState(no),attemptId=currentAttemptId(no),attempt=state.cleaningAttempts?.[attemptId];
        return state.role==='maid'&&!isLocked()&&(signedInMaidIsActive()||maidCanCompleteRequiredReclean(no))&&room?.assignee===signedInMaidName()&&task.attemptId===attemptId&&attempt?.performerId===signedInMaidId()&&['active','reclean'].includes(attempt.status)&&['scheduled','claimed','reclean'].includes(job)&&attemptAccessStatus(no,attempt).allowed;
      }
      function maidCanEditCleaning(no,allowedJobs=['cleaning']) {

      function renderRoomCleaningFallbackPanel(no) {
        return `<section class="card card-pad"><div class="section-head"><h3>청소 작업</h3>${statusBadge(statusLabel(state.jobs[no]),roomNeedsCleaningNow(no)?'amber':'neutral')}</div>${renderManualCleaningToggle(no)}</section>`;
      }
      function mergeRoomCleaningPanel(html,no) {
        if(html.includes(`data-room-cleaning-panel="${no}"`))return html;
        const marker='<div class="detail-stack">',index=html.indexOf(marker);
        return index<0?html:html.slice(0,index+marker.length)+renderRoomCleaningFallbackPanel(no)+html.slice(index+marker.length);
      }
      function renderRoomDetail(no) {
        const room=ROOMS.find(item=>item.no===no);
        if(roomIsOnHold(no))return mergeRoomBasicsPanel(mergeRoomCleaningPanel(renderCatalogRoomDetail(no),no),no);
        if(no==='332'&&['active','resolved'].includes(state.conflict)) {
          return mergeRoomBasicsPanel(mergeRoomCleaningPanel(mergeRoomIssuesPanel(mergeRoomOperationPanel(renderRoomConflict332(),no),no),no),no);
        }
        return mergeRoomBasicsPanel(mergeRoomCleaningPanel(mergeRoomIssuesPanel(mergeRoomOperationPanel(renderRoomDetailStandard(no),no),no),no),no);
      }

      function taskRequirements(no) {
        const task=taskState(no),snapshot=task.templateSnapshot||snapshotForAttempt(no,state.cleaningAttempts?.[task.attemptId]),requiredUploads=task.uploads.filter(upload=>upload.required),requiredDone=requiredUploads.every(upload=>upload.status==='done'),failed=task.uploads.some(upload=>upload.status==='failed'||upload.status==='uploading');
        return {task,snapshot,requiredDone,failed,requiredUploads};
      }
      function taskUploadByIdentity(no,photoId,attemptId) {
        const task=state.taskInputs?.[no];
        if(!attemptId||task?.attemptId!==attemptId)return null;
        const upload=task.uploads?.find(item=>item.id===photoId);
        return upload?{task,upload}:null;
      }
      function taskUploadForMutation(no,photoId,attemptId,allowedJobs=['cleaning']) {
        if(!maidCanEditCleaning(no,allowedJobs))return null;
        const resolved=taskUploadByIdentity(no,photoId,attemptId),currentId=currentAttemptId(no);
        return resolved&&currentId===attemptId?resolved:null;
      }
      function taskPhotoInputId(no,photoId,source) { return `task-photo-${source}-${no}-${photoId}`.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g,'-'); }
      function taskZoneGroups(task) {
        const groups=[],byZone=new Map();
        task.uploads.forEach(upload=>{
          const zone=upload.zone||'사진';
          if(!byZone.has(zone)){const group={zone,uploads:[]};byZone.set(zone,group);groups.push(group);}
          byZone.get(zone).uploads.push(upload);
        });
        return groups;
      }
      function taskUploadStatusText(upload) {
        if(upload.status==='uploading')return '사진 확인 중';
        if(upload.status==='failed')return '전송 실패 · 다시 촬영';
        if(upload.status==='done')return upload.image?.objectUrl?'현장 촬영 완료':'사진 완료';
        return upload.required?'필수 사진 없음':'선택 사진 없음';
      }
      function taskPhotoVisual(upload) {
        if(upload.image?.objectUrl)return `<img src="${esc(upload.image.src)}" alt="${esc(upload.label)} 현장 촬영 미리보기">`;
        if(['done','failed'].includes(upload.status)&&upload.image)return inspectionPhotoSvg(upload,upload.label);
        return icon(upload.status==='uploading'?'clock':'camera');
      }

        }).join('')}</div>`;
        return `<section class="task-zone-card ${complete?'complete':''} ${failed?'failed':''}" aria-labelledby="task-zone-${no}-${groupsafe(group.zone)}"><div class="task-zone-head"><div><h4 id="task-zone-${no}-${groupsafe(group.zone)}">${esc(group.zone)}</h4><p>필수 ${requiredUploads.length}장 · 선택 ${group.uploads.length-requiredUploads.length}장</p></div>${statusBadge(complete?'구역 완료':failed?'사진 확인 필요':hasRequired?'촬영 필요':'선택',complete?'green':failed?'red':hasRequired?'amber':'neutral')}</div>${photos}</section>`;

        const activeAttempt=state.cleaningAttempts?.[currentAttemptId(no)],activeGuestCount=guestCountForAttempt(activeAttempt),activeCheckout=activeAttempt?.checkoutSnapshot||room.checkout,activeCheckin=activeAttempt?.checkinSnapshot||room.checkin,activeRoomMeta=activeAttempt?.roomMetaSnapshot||roomMetadataSnapshot(no),activeType=ROOM_TYPES[activeRoomMeta?.typeId]||ROOM_TYPES[room.type],activeTypeName=activeRoomMeta?.typeName||activeType.name,activeTemplate=snapshotForAttempt(no,activeAttempt),activeRate=activeAttempt?.baseRateSnapshot??activeType.rate,activeKind=activeAttempt?.kind||(job==='reclean'?'재청소':'퇴실 청소'),activeMinutes=activeTemplate?.minutes??activeType.minutes,activeStageLabel=activeKind==='재청소'?(job==='reclean'?'재청소 · 시작 예정':`재청소 · ${statusLabel(job)}`):cleaningLabel(job);
        const maidOwnsSubmission=['inspection','approved'].includes(job)&&currentSubmission(no)?.performerId===signedInMaidId();
        if(state.role==='maid'&&room.assignee!==signedInMaidName()&&!maidOwnsSubmission)return renderCoach()+detailHeader('접근할 수 없는 업무','본인에게 배정되었거나 본인이 제출한 객실만 확인할 수 있습니다.')+'<section class="card card-pad"><div class="notice notice-danger"><div><strong>업무 접근이 차단됐습니다.</strong><br>목록으로 돌아가 관리자에게 배정받은 업무를 확인해 주세요.</div></div></section>';
        if(job==='approved'&&!currentSubmission(no))return renderInspectionDetail(no);
        if(state.role==='admin'&&(['inspection','approved'].includes(job)||(state.inspections[no]==='rejected'&&state.detail?.mode!=='reclean'))) return renderInspectionDetail(no);
        const access=attemptAccessStatus(no,activeAttempt),rollover=rolloverMetaForRoom(no),start=access.start, reached=access.allowed, primary=cleaningPrimary(no,job), occupied=room.occupancy==='occupied', special=roomReservationStatus(room), preparationDeadline=activeAttempt?.deadlineSnapshot||shiftClockTime(special.checkin,-30)||'마감 없음';
        if(state.role==='admin') return renderCoach()+renderNetworkNotice()+detailHeader(`${no}호 · ${activeStageLabel}`,`${activeKind} · ${activeTypeName} · ${money(activeRate)} · ${activeMinutes}분 · ${activeTemplate?.version||'데모'}`)+`<div class="detail-grid"><div class="detail-stack"><section class="card card-pad"><div class="section-head"><h3>작업 상태</h3>${statusBadge(statusLabel(job),['cleaning','upload'].includes(job)?'amber':'neutral')}</div>${rolloverBadgeMarkup(rollover)}<div class="info-grid"><div class="info-item"><span>담당</span><strong>${esc(room.assignee)}</strong></div><div class="info-item"><span>시작 가능</span><strong>${start}</strong></div>${rollover?`<div class="info-item"><span>계획 서비스일</span><strong>${esc(dateLabel(rollover.planDate))}</strong></div><div class="info-item"><span>현재 수행일</span><strong>${esc(dateLabel(rollover.effectiveDate))}</strong></div>`:''}<div class="info-item"><span>숙박 인원</span><strong>${activeGuestCount?guestCountLabel(activeGuestCount):'미기록'}</strong></div><div class="info-item"><span>이전 예약 체크아웃 → 다음 예약 체크인</span><strong>${esc(activeCheckout)} → ${esc(activeCheckin)}</strong></div><div class="info-item"><span>현재 단계</span><strong>${esc(activeStageLabel)}</strong></div></div>${activeKind==='재청소'?`<div class="notice notice-info" style="margin-top:12px"><div><strong>처음 청소한 본인에게 고정된 무급 재청소입니다.</strong><br>담당 취소·이관·별도 보상·폭탄방 추가요금 없이 완료 이력만 남깁니다.</div></div>`:''}${['public','draft','future','scheduled'].includes(job)?`<div style="margin-top:12px">${button('메이드 직접 배정','direct-assign','primary',`data-id="${no}"`)}</div>`:''}</section>${job==='upload'?`<section class="card card-pad">${renderTaskInputSections(no,job)}</section>`:''}</div><aside class="detail-stack"><section class="card card-pad"><h3>수행·제출 타임라인</h3>${renderCleaningTimeline(no,job)}</section></aside></div>`;
        const activeCleaning=activeCleaningFor();
        return renderCoach()+renderNetworkNotice()+detailHeader(`${no}호 · ${activeStageLabel}`,`${activeKind} · ${activeTypeName} · ${money(activeRate)} · ${activeMinutes}분 ·       }
      function mergeRoomCleaningPanel(html,no) {
        if(html.includes(`data-room-cleaning-panel="${no}"`))return html;
        const marker='<div class="detail-stack">',index=html.indexOf(marker);
        return index<0?html:html.slice(0,index+marker.length)+renderRoomCleaningFallbackPanel(no)+html.slice(index+marker.length);
      }
      function renderRoomDetail(no) {
        const room=ROOMS.find(item=>item.no===no);
        if(roomIsOnHold(no))return mergeRoomBasicsPanel(mergeRoomCleaningPanel(renderCatalogRoomDetail(no),no),no);
        if(no==='332'&&['active','resolved'].includes(state.conflict)) {
          return mergeRoomBasicsPanel(mergeRoomCleaningPanel(mergeRoomIssuesPanel(mergeRoomOperationPanel(renderRoomConflict332(),no),no),no),no);
        }
        return mergeRoomBasicsPanel(mergeRoomCleaningPanel(mergeRoomIssuesPanel(mergeRoomOperationPanel(renderRoomDetailStandard(no),no),no),no),no);
      }

      function taskRequirements(no) {
        const task=taskState(no),snapshot=task.templateSnapshot||snapshotForAttempt(no,state.cleaningAttempts?.[task.attemptId]),requiredUploads=task.uploads.filter(upload=>upload.required),requiredDone=requiredUploads.every(upload=>upload.status==='done'),failed=task.uploads.some(upload=>upload.status==='failed'||upload.status==='uploading');
        return {task,snapshot,requiredDone,failed,requiredUploads};
      }
      function taskUploadByIdentity(no,photoId,attemptId) {
        const task=state.taskInputs?.[no];
        if(!attemptId||task?.attemptId!==attemptId)return null;
        const upload=task.uploads?.find(item=>item.id===photoId);
        return upload?{task,upload}:null;
      }
      function taskUploadForMutation(no,photoId,attemptId,allowedJobs=['cleaning']) {
        if(!maidCanEditCleaning(no,allowedJobs))return null;
        const resolved=taskUploadByIdentity(no,photoId,attemptId),currentId=currentAttemptId(no);
        return resolved&&currentId===attemptId?resolved:null;
      }
      function taskPhotoInputId(no,photoId,source) { return `task-photo-${source}-${no}-${photoId}`.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g,'-'); }
      function taskZoneGroups(task) {
        const groups=[],byZone=new Map();
        task.uploads.forEach(upload=>{
          const zone=upload.zone||'사진';
          if(!byZone.has(zone)){const group={zone,uploads:[]};byZone.set(zone,group);groups.push(group);}
          byZone.get(zone).uploads.push(upload);
        });
        return groups;
      }
      function taskUploadStatusText(upload) {
        if(upload.status==='uploading')return '사진 확인 중';
        if(upload.status==='failed')return '전송 실패 · 다시 촬영';
        if(upload.status==='done')return upload.image?.objectUrl?'현장 촬영 완료':'사진 완료';
        return upload.required?'필수 사진 없음':'선택 사진 없음';
      }
      function taskPhotoVisual(upload) {
        if(upload.image?.objectUrl)return `<img src="${esc(upload.image.src)}" alt="${esc(upload.label)} 현장 촬영 미리보기">`;
        if(['done','failed'].includes(upload.status)&&upload.image)return inspectionPhotoSvg(upload,upload.label);
        return icon(upload.status==='uploading'?'clock':'camera');
      }
      function taskZoneCardMarkup(no,group,task,editable) {
        const attemptId=task.attemptId||'',requiredUploads=group.uploads.filter(item=>item.required),photosDone=requiredUploads.every(upload=>upload.status==='done'),failed=group.uploads.some(upload=>upload.status==='failed'||upload.status==='uploading'),hasRequired=requiredUploads.length>0,complete=hasRequired&&photosDone&&!failed;
        const photos=`<div class="task-zone-photos">${group.uploads.map(upload=>{
          const cameraId=taskPhotoInputId(no,upload.id,'camera'),galleryId=taskPhotoInputId(no,upload.id,'gallery'),busy=upload.status==='uploading',captured=upload.status==='done'||upload.status==='failed'||!!upload.image?.objectUrl;
          return `<article class="task-zone-photo ${upload.status}" data-photo-slot="${esc(upload.id)}"><div class="task-zone-photo-main"><span class="task-zone-photo-visual">${taskPhotoVisual(upload)}</span><div class="task-zone-photo-copy"><strong>${esc(upload.label)} · ${upload.required?'필수':'선택'}</strong><span>${esc(upload.description||'청소 완료 상태를 촬영합니다.')}</span><span class="photo-state" aria-live="polite">${esc(taskUploadStatusText(upload))}${upload.instanceCount>1?` · ${upload.instance}/${upload.instanceCount}`:''}</span></div></div>${editable?`<div class="task-zone-photo-actions"><button class="btn btn-primary" type="button" data-action="capture-task-photo" data-room="${no}" data-photo="${esc(upload.id)}" data-attempt="${esc(attemptId)}" aria-label="${no}호 ${esc(group.zone)} ${esc(upload.label)} ${captured?'다시 촬영':'바로 촬영'}" ${busy?'disabled':''}>${icon('camera','icon-sm')}${captured?'다시 촬영':'바로 촬영'}</button><button class="btn btn-outline" type="button" data-action="choose-task-photo" data-room="${no}" data-photo="${esc(upload.id)}" data-attempt="${esc(attemptId)}" aria-label="${no}호 ${esc(group.zone)} ${esc(upload.label)} 갤러리에서 선택" ${busy?'disabled':''}>갤러리</button>${captured?`<button class="btn btn-outline icon-only" type="button" data-action="remove-task-photo" data-room="${no}" data-photo="${esc(upload.id)}" data-attempt="${esc(attemptId)}" aria-label="${no}호 ${esc(group.zone)} ${esc(upload.label)} 사진 삭제" ${busy?'disabled':''}>${icon('x','icon-sm')}</button>`:'<span aria-hidden="true"></span>'}</div><input id="${cameraId}" hidden type="file" accept="image/*" capture="environment" data-control="task-photo-file" data-source="camera" data-room="${no}" data-photo="${esc(upload.id)}" data-attempt="${esc(attemptId)}"><input id="${galleryId}" hidden type="file" accept="image/*" data-control="task-photo-file" data-source="gallery" data-room="${no}" data-photo="${esc(upload.id)}" data-attempt="${esc(attemptId)}">`:''}</article>`;
        }).join('')}</div>`;
        return `<section class="task-zone-card ${complete?'complete':''} ${failed?'failed':''}" aria-labelledby="task-zone-${no}-${groupsafe(group.zone)}"><div class="task-zone-head"><div><h4 id="task-zone-${no}-${groupsafe(group.zone)}">${esc(group.zone)}</h4><p>필수 ${requiredUploads.length}장 · 선택 ${group.uploads.length-requiredUploads.length}장</p></div>${statusBadge(complete?'구역 완료':failed?'사진 확인 필요':hasRequired?'촬영 필요':'선택',complete?'green':failed?'red':hasRequired?'amber':'neutral')}</div>${photos}</section>`;
      }
      function groupsafe(value) { return String(value||'zone').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9가-힣_-]/g,'-'); }
      function renderRoomIssueInput(no,editable) {
        const draft=roomIssueDraft(no),records=visibleRoomIssueRecords(no),hasDraft=!!draft.note.trim()||draft.photos.length>0;
        const types=['냄새','청소 미흡','시설 고장','비품 부족·파손','분실물','기타'];
        const editor=editable?`<div class="room-issue-form"><div class="notice notice-warning" style="margin:0"><div><strong>개인정보 입력 금지</strong><br>고객 이름·휴대폰 번호·이메일은 메모와 사진 파일명에 남기지 않습니다.</div></div><div class="room-issue-form-grid"><div class="field"><label for="room-issue-type-${no}">특이사항 유형 · 선택</label><select id="room-issue-type-${no}" class="select-control" data-control="room-issue-type" data-room="${no}"><option value="">유형 선택 안 함</option>${types.map(type=>`<option value="${type}" ${draft.type===type?'selected':''}>${type}</option>`).join('')}</select></div><div class="field"><label for="room-issue-note-${no}">업무 메모 · 선택</label><textarea id="room-issue-note-${no}" class="input-control task-note" rows="3" data-control="room-issue-note" data-room="${no}" placeholder="개인정보를 제외한 객실 상태를 적어 주세요.">${esc(draft.note)}</textarea></div></div><div class="room-issue-picker"><div class="room-issue-picker-copy"><strong>이미지 여러 장</strong><span>한 번에 여러 이미지를 선택하고 등록 전 개별 제거할 수 있습니다.</span></div><input id="room-issue-files-${no}" class="sr-only" type="file" accept="image/*" multiple tabindex="-1" data-control="room-issue-files" data-room="${no}"><button class="btn btn-outline" type="button" data-action="choose-room-issue-files" data-room="${no}">${icon('camera','icon-sm')}이미지 선택</button></div>${renderRoomIssueDraftPhotos(no)}<button class="btn btn-primary btn-block" type="button" data-action="save-room-issue" data-room="${no}" ${hasDraft?'':'disabled'}>특이사항 등록 · 선택 ${draft.photos.length}장</button><p class="audit-note" style="margin:0">현재 탭 메모리에서만 미리보기를 유지하는 데모이며 실제 서버 업로드·영속 저장이 아닙니다.</p></div>`:'<p class="audit-note" style="margin:12px 0 0">메이드는 청소 중에만 특이사항을 등록할 수 있습니다. 저장된 기록은 관리자에게 그대로 공유됩니다.</p>';
        return `${editor}${records.length?renderRoomIssueRecords(no):editable?'':'<div class="inline-empty"><h3>등록된 특이사항이 없습니다</h3><p>메이드가 저장한 특이사항과 사진이 여기에 표시됩니다.</p></div>'}`;
      }
      function renderCleaningInputSection(no,key,title,meta,body) {
        const titleId=`cleaning-section-${groupsafe(no)}-${groupsafe(key)}`;
        return `<section class="cleaning-section" data-cleaning-section="${esc(key)}" aria-labelledby="${titleId}"><div class="cleaning-section-head"><h3 id="${titleId}">${esc(title)}</h3><span data-cleaning-section-meta>${esc(meta)}</span></div><div class="cleaning-section-body">${body}</div></section>`;
      }
      function renderTaskInputSections(no,job) {
        const {task,snapshot,requiredUploads}=taskRequirements(no), occupied=ROOMS.find(r=>r.no===no)?.occupancy==='occupied';
        const editable=maidCanEditRoomIssue(no,job),bomb=bombRoomReport(no),bombMeta=bombRoomStatusMeta(bomb),groups=taskZoneGroups(task);
        const doneUploads=task.uploads.filter(upload=>upload.status==='done').length,type=ROOM_TYPES[snapshot?.typeId||ROOMS.find(room=>room.no===no)?.type||'standard'];
        const requiredGroups=groups.filter(group=>group.uploads.some(item=>item.required)),completedGroups=requiredGroups.filter(group=>group.uploads.filter(item=>item.required).every(item=>item.status==='done')).length;
        const tvSafety=snapshot?.photos?.some(item=>item.id==='tv-on'||String(item.id).startsWith('tv-on-'))?' TV는 켜고 계정·QR·알림 없는 기본 화면으로 촬영하세요.':'';
        const photoBody=`<div class="photo-template-banner"><div><strong>${esc(type.name)} · ${esc(snapshot?.name||'퇴실 청소')}</strong><span>필수 사진 ${requiredUploads.length}장 · 구역 버튼을 눌러 바로 촬영하세요.</span></div><div class="badge-row">${statusBadge(`${completedGroups}/${requiredGroups.length} 구역 완료`,completedGroups===requiredGroups.length?'green':'amber')}</div></div><div class="task-zone-progress"><div><span>완료 구역</span><strong>${completedGroups}/${requiredGroups.length}</strong></div><div><span>사진 촬영</span><strong>${doneUploads}/${task.uploads.length}</strong></div></div><div class="task-zone-safety">${icon('shield','icon-sm')}<span><strong>촬영 전 개인정보를 확인하세요.</strong><br>연락처·얼굴·고객 물품·거울 속 사람은 제외하세요.${tvSafety}</span></div><div class="task-zone-grid">${groups.map(group=>taskZoneCardMarkup(no,group,task,editable)).join('')}</div>`;
        const candleBody=`<p class="audit-note" style="margin:12px 0 0">청소 결과를 입력하는 동안만 객실의 최종 촛불 수량을 조정할 수 있습니다.</p><div class="candle-stepper" role="group" aria-label="${no}호 청소 결과 촛불 수량"><button class="btn btn-outline" type="button" data-action="task-candle-change" data-room="${no}" data-delta="-1" aria-label="촛불 1개 줄이기" ${!editable||task.candle<1?'disabled':''}>−</button><div class="candle-stepper-value" aria-live="polite"><strong>${task.candle||0}개</strong><span>청소 완료 후 객실 수량</span></div><button class="btn btn-outline" type="button" data-action="task-candle-change" data-room="${no}" data-delta="1" aria-label="촛불 1개 늘리기" ${!editable||task.candle>=9?'disabled':''}>+</button></div>`;
        return `<div class="cleaning-sections">${renderCleaningInputSection(no,'photos','구역별 사진 촬영',`${completedGroups}/${requiredGroups.length} 구역`,photoBody)}${renderCleaningInputSection(no,'bomb-room','폭탄방 신고',bombMeta.label,renderBombRoomPanel(no,job))}${renderCleaningInputSection(no,'room-issues','객실 특이사항',`${visibleRoomIssueRecords(no).length}건 · 선택 ${task.issueDraft.photos.length}장`,renderRoomIssueInput(no,editable))}${occupied?'':renderCleaningInputSection(no,'candles','촛불',`${task.candle||0}개`,candleBody)}</div>`;
      }
      function cleaningPrimary(no,job) {
        const req=taskRequirements(no),access=attemptAccessStatus(no);
        if(['scheduled','claimed','reclean'].includes(job)) return {label:access.allowed?(job==='reclean'?'재청소 시작':'일 시작'):access.reason,action:'start-cleaning',disabled:!access.allowed||isLocked()};
        if(job==='cleaning') { const photosLeft=req.requiredUploads.filter(item=>item.status!=='done').length;return {label:photosLeft?`남은 필수 사진 ${photosLeft}장`:'현장 완료',action:'field-complete-v2',disabled:!!photosLeft||req.failed||isLocked()}; }
        if(job==='upload'&&req.failed) return {label:'미전송 재시도',action:'retry-all-task-photos',disabled:isLocked()};
        if(job==='upload') return {label:'청소 전체 제출',action:'submit-cleaning-v2',disabled:!req.requiredDone||isLocked()};
        if(job==='inspection'||job==='approved') return {label:'제출 결과 보기',action:'demo-info',disabled:false};
        return {label:'상세 확인',action:'demo-info',disabled:false};
      }
      function maidCanStart(no) {
        const room=ROOMS.find(r=>r.no===no),job=state.jobs[no],task=taskState(no),attemptId=currentAttemptId(no),attempt=state.cleaningAttempts?.[attemptId];
        return state.role==='maid'&&!isLocked()&&(signedInMaidIsActive()||maidCanCompleteRequiredReclean(no))&&room?.assignee===signedInMaidName()&&task.attemptId===attemptId&&attempt?.performerId===signedInMaidId()&&['active','reclean'].includes(attempt.status)&&['scheduled','claimed','reclean'].includes(job)&&attemptAccessStatus(no,attempt).allowed;
      }

      function renderRoomDetail(no) {
        const room=ROOMS.find(item=>item.no===no);
        if(roomIsOnHold(no))return mergeRoomBasicsPanel(mergeRoomCleaningPanel(renderCatalogRoomDetail(no),no),no);
        if(no==='332'&&['active','resolved'].includes(state.conflict)) {
          return mergeRoomBasicsPanel(mergeRoomCleaningPanel(mergeRoomIssuesPanel(mergeRoomOperationPanel(renderRoomConflict332(),no),no),no),no);
        }
        return mergeRoomBasicsPanel(mergeRoomCleaningPanel(mergeRoomIssuesPanel(mergeRoomOperationPanel(renderRoomDetailStandard(no),no),no),no),no);
      }

      function taskRequirements(no) {
        const task=taskState(no),snapshot=task.templateSnapshot||snapshotForAttempt(no,state.cleaningAttempts?.[task.attemptId]),requiredUploads=task.uploads.filter(upload=>upload.required),requiredDone=requiredUploads.every(upload=>upload.status==='done'),failed=task.uploads.some(upload=>upload.status==='failed'||upload.status==='uploading');
        return {task,snapshot,requiredDone,failed,requiredUploads};
      }
      function taskUploadByIdentity(no,photoId,attemptId) {
        const task=state.taskInputs?.[no];
        if(!attemptId||task?.attemptId!==attemptId)return null;
        const upload=task.uploads?.find(item=>item.id===photoId);
        return upload?{task,upload}:null;
      }
      function taskUploadForMutation(no,photoId,attemptId,allowedJobs=['cleaning']) {
        if(!maidCanEditCleaning(no,allowedJobs))return null;
        const resolved=taskUploadByIdentity(no,photoId,attemptId),currentId=currentAttemptId(no);
        return resolved&&currentId===attemptId?resolved:null;
      }
      function taskPhotoInputId(no,photoId,source) { return `task-photo-${source}-${no}-${photoId}`.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g,'-'); }
      function taskZoneGroups(task) {
        const groups=[],byZone=new Map();
        task.uploads.forEach(upload=>{
          const zone=upload.zone||'사진';
          if(!byZone.has(zone)){const group={zone,uploads:[]};byZone.set(zone,group);groups.push(group);}
          byZone.get(zone).uploads.push(upload);
        });
        return groups;
      }
      function taskUploadStatusText(upload) {
        if(upload.status==='uploading')return '사진 확인 중';
        if(upload.status==='failed')return '전송 실패 · 다시 촬영';
        if(upload.status==='done')return upload.image?.objectUrl?'현장 촬영 완료':'사진 완료';
        return upload.required?'필수 사진 없음':'선택 사진 없음';
      }
      function taskPhotoVisual(upload) {
        if(upload.image?.objectUrl)return `<img src="${esc(upload.image.src)}" alt="${esc(upload.label)} 현장 촬영 미리보기">`;
        if(['done','failed'].includes(upload.status)&&upload.image)return inspectionPhotoSvg(upload,upload.label);
        return icon(upload.status==='uploading'?'clock':'camera');
      }
      function taskZoneCardMarkup(no,group,task,editable) {
        const attemptId=task.attemptId||'',requiredUploads=group.uploads.filter(item=>item.required),photosDone=requiredUploads.every(upload=>upload.status==='done'),failed=group.uploads.some(upload=>upload.status==='failed'||upload.status==='uploading'),hasRequired=requiredUploads.length>0,complete=hasRequired&&photosDone&&!failed;
        const photos=`<div class="task-zone-photos">${group.uploads.map(upload=>{
          const cameraId=taskPhotoInputId(no,upload.id,'camera'),galleryId=taskPhotoInputId(no,upload.id,'gallery'),busy=upload.status==='uploading',captured=upload.status==='done'||upload.status==='failed'||!!upload.image?.objectUrl;
          return `<article class="task-zone-photo ${upload.status}" data-photo-slot="${esc(upload.id)}"><div class="task-zone-photo-main"><span class="task-zone-photo-visual">${taskPhotoVisual(upload)}</span><div class="task-zone-photo-copy"><strong>${esc(upload.label)} · ${upload.required?'필수':'선택'}</strong><span>${esc(upload.description||'청소 완료 상태를 촬영합니다.')}</span><span class="photo-state" aria-live="polite">${esc(taskUploadStatusText(upload))}${upload.instanceCount>1?` · ${upload.instance}/${upload.instanceCount}`:''}</span></div></div>${editable?`<div class="task-zone-photo-actions"><button class="btn btn-primary" type="button" data-action="capture-task-photo" data-room="${no}" data-photo="${esc(upload.id)}" data-attempt="${esc(attemptId)}" aria-label="${no}호 ${esc(group.zone)} ${esc(upload.label)} ${captured?'다시 촬영':'바로 촬영'}" ${busy?'disabled':''}>${icon('camera','icon-sm')}${captured?'다시 촬영':'바로 촬영'}</button><button class="btn btn-outline" type="button" data-action="choose-task-photo" data-room="${no}" data-photo="${esc(upload.id)}" data-attempt="${esc(attemptId)}" aria-label="${no}호 ${esc(group.zone)} ${esc(upload.label)} 갤러리에서 선택" ${busy?'disabled':''}>갤러리</button>${captured?`<button class="btn btn-outline icon-only" type="button" data-action="remove-task-photo" data-room="${no}" data-photo="${esc(upload.id)}" data-attempt="${esc(attemptId)}" aria-label="${no}호 ${esc(group.zone)} ${esc(upload.label)} 사진 삭제" ${busy?'disabled':''}>${icon('x','icon-sm')}</button>`:'<span aria-hidden="true"></span>'}</div><input id="${cameraId}" hidden type="file" accept="image/*" capture="environment" data-control="task-photo-file" data-source="camera" data-room="${no}" data-photo="${esc(upload.id)}" data-attempt="${esc(attemptId)}"><input id="${galleryId}" hidden type="file" accept="image/*" data-control="task-photo-file" data-source="gallery" data-room="${no}" data-photo="${esc(upload.id)}" data-attempt="${esc(attemptId)}">`:''}</article>`;
        }).join('')}</div>`;
        return `<section class="task-zone-card ${complete?'complete':''} ${failed?'failed':''}" aria-labelledby="task-zone-${no}-${groupsafe(group.zone)}"><div class="task-zone-head"><div><h4 id="task-zone-${no}-${groupsafe(group.zone)}">${esc(group.zone)}</h4><p>필수 ${requiredUploads.length}장 · 선택 ${group.uploads.length-requiredUploads.length}장</p></div>${statusBadge(complete?'구역 완료':failed?'사진 확인 필요':hasRequired?'촬영 필요':'선택',complete?'green':failed?'red':hasRequired?'amber':'neutral')}</div>${photos}</section>`;
      }
      function groupsafe(value) { return String(value||'zone').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9가-힣_-]/g,'-'); }

      function cleaningPrimary(no,job) {

      function taskRequirements(no) {
        const task=taskState(no),snapshot=task.templateSnapshot||snapshotForAttempt(no,state.cleaningAttempts?.[task.attemptId]),requiredUploads=task.uploads.filter(upload=>upload.required),requiredDone=requiredUploads.every(upload=>upload.status==='done'),failed=task.uploads.some(upload=>upload.status==='failed'||upload.status==='uploading');
        return {task,snapshot,requiredDone,failed,requiredUploads};
      }
      function taskUploadByIdentity(no,photoId,attemptId) {
        const task=state.taskInputs?.[no];
        if(!attemptId||task?.attemptId!==attemptId)return null;
        const upload=task.uploads?.find(item=>item.id===photoId);
        return upload?{task,upload}:null;
      }
      function taskUploadForMutation(no,photoId,attemptId,allowedJobs=['cleaning']) {
        if(!maidCanEditCleaning(no,allowedJobs))return null;
        const resolved=taskUploadByIdentity(no,photoId,attemptId),currentId=currentAttemptId(no);
        return resolved&&currentId===attemptId?resolved:null;
      }
      function taskPhotoInputId(no,photoId,source) { return `task-photo-${source}-${no}-${photoId}`.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g,'-'); }
      function taskZoneGroups(task) {
        const groups=[],byZone=new Map();
        task.uploads.forEach(upload=>{
          const zone=upload.zone||'사진';
          if(!byZone.has(zone)){const group={zone,uploads:[]};byZone.set(zone,group);groups.push(group);}
          byZone.get(zone).uploads.push(upload);
        });
        return groups;
      }
      function taskUploadStatusText(upload) {
        if(upload.status==='uploading')return '사진 확인 중';
        if(upload.status==='failed')return '전송 실패 · 다시 촬영';
        if(upload.status==='done')return upload.image?.objectUrl?'현장 촬영 완료':'사진 완료';
        return upload.required?'필수 사진 없음':'선택 사진 없음';
      }
      function taskPhotoVisual(upload) {
        if(upload.image?.objectUrl)return `<img src="${esc(upload.image.src)}" alt="${esc(upload.label)} 현장 촬영 미리보기">`;
        if(['done','failed'].includes(upload.status)&&upload.image)return inspectionPhotoSvg(upload,upload.label);
        return icon(upload.status==='uploading'?'clock':'camera');
      }

        const photos=`<div class="task-zone-photos">${group.uploads.map(upload=>{
          const cameraId=taskPhotoInputId(no,upload.id,'camera'),galleryId=taskPhotoInputId(no,upload.id,'gallery'),busy=upload.status==='uploading',captured=upload.status==='done'||upload.status==='failed'||!!upload.image?.objectUrl;
          return `<article class="task-zone-photo ${upload.status}" data-photo-slot="${esc(upload.id)}"><div class="task-zone-photo-main"><span class="task-zone-photo-visual">${taskPhotoVisual(upload)}</span><div class="task-zone-photo-copy"><strong>${esc(upload.label)} · ${upload.required?'필수':'선택'}</strong><span>${esc(upload.description||'청소 완료 상태를 촬영합니다.')}</span><span class="photo-state" aria-live="polite">${esc(taskUploadStatusText(upload))}${upload.instanceCount>1?` · ${upload.instance}/${upload.instanceCount}`:''}</span></div></div>${editable?`<div class="task-zone-photo-actions"><button class="btn btn-primary" type="button" data-action="capture-task-photo" data-room="${no}" data-photo="${esc(upload.id)}" data-attempt="${esc(attemptId)}" aria-label="${no}호 ${esc(group.zone)} ${esc(upload.label)} ${captured?'다시 촬영':'바로 촬영'}" ${busy?'disabled':''}>${icon('camera','icon-sm')}${captured?'다시 촬영':'바로 촬영'}</button><button class="btn btn-outline" type="button" data-action="choose-task-photo" data-room="${no}" data-photo="${esc(upload.id)}" data-attempt="${esc(attemptId)}" aria-label="${no}호 ${esc(group.zone)} ${esc(upload.label)} 갤러리에서 선택" ${busy?'disabled':''}>갤러리</button>${captured?`<button class="btn btn-outline icon-only" type="button" data-action="remove-task-photo" data-room="${no}" data-photo="${esc(upload.id)}" data-attempt="${esc(attemptId)}" aria-label="${no}호 ${esc(group.zone)} ${esc(upload.label)} 사진 삭제" ${busy?'disabled':''}>${icon('x','icon-sm')}</button>`:'<span aria-hidden="true"></span>'}</div><input id="${cameraId}" hidden type="file" accept="image/*" capture="environment" data-control="task-photo-file" data-source="camera" data-room="${no}" data-photo="${esc(upload.id)}" data-attempt="${esc(attemptId)}"><input id="${galleryId}" hidden type="file" accept="image/*" data-control="task-photo-file" data-source="gallery" data-room="${no}" data-photo="${esc(upload.id)}" data-attempt="${esc(attemptId)}">`:''}</article>`;
        }).join('')}</div>`;
        return `<section class="task-zone-card ${complete?'complete':''} ${failed?'failed':''}" aria-labelledby="task-zone-${no}-${groupsafe(group.zone)}"><div class="task-zone-head"><div><h4 id="task-zone-${no}-${groupsafe(group.zone)}">${esc(group.zone)}</h4><p>필수 ${requiredUploads.length}장 · 선택 ${group.uploads.length-requiredUploads.length}장</p></div>${statusBadge(complete?'구역 완료':failed?'사진 확인 필요':hasRequired?'촬영 필요':'선택',complete?'green':failed?'red':hasRequired?'amber':'neutral')}</div>${photos}</section>`;
      }
      function groupsafe(value) { return String(value||'zone').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9가-힣_-]/g,'-'); }

      function renderTaskInputSections(no,job) {

      function taskRequirements(no) {
        const task=taskState(no),snapshot=task.templateSnapshot||snapshotForAttempt(no,state.cleaningAttempts?.[task.attemptId]),requiredUploads=task.uploads.filter(upload=>upload.required),requiredDone=requiredUploads.every(upload=>upload.status==='done'),failed=task.uploads.some(upload=>upload.status==='failed'||upload.status==='uploading');
        return {task,snapshot,requiredDone,failed,requiredUploads};
      }
      function taskUploadByIdentity(no,photoId,attemptId) {
        const task=state.taskInputs?.[no];
        if(!attemptId||task?.attemptId!==attemptId)return null;
        const upload=task.uploads?.find(item=>item.id===photoId);
        return upload?{task,upload}:null;
      }
      function taskUploadForMutation(no,photoId,attemptId,allowedJobs=['cleaning']) {
        if(!maidCanEditCleaning(no,allowedJobs))return null;
        const resolved=taskUploadByIdentity(no,photoId,attemptId),currentId=currentAttemptId(no);
        return resolved&&currentId===attemptId?resolved:null;
      }
      function taskPhotoInputId(no,photoId,source) { return `task-photo-${source}-${no}-${photoId}`.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g,'-'); }
      function taskZoneGroups(task) {
        const groups=[],byZone=new Map();
        task.uploads.forEach(upload=>{
          const zone=upload.zone||'사진';
          if(!byZone.has(zone)){const group={zone,uploads:[]};byZone.set(zone,group);groups.push(group);}
          byZone.get(zone).uploads.push(upload);
        });
        return groups;
      }
      function taskUploadStatusText(upload) {
        if(upload.status==='uploading')return '사진 확인 중';
        if(upload.status==='failed')return '전송 실패 · 다시 촬영';
        if(upload.status==='done')return upload.image?.objectUrl?'현장 촬영 완료':'사진 완료';
        return upload.required?'필수 사진 없음':'선택 사진 없음';
      }

      function cleaningPrimary(no,job) {
        const req=taskRequirements(no),access=attemptAccessStatus(no);
        if(['scheduled','claimed','reclean'].includes(job)) return {label:access.allowed?(job==='reclean'?'재청소 시작':'일 시작'):access.reason,action:'start-cleaning',disabled:!access.allowed||isLocked()};
        if(job==='cleaning') { const photosLeft=req.requiredUploads.filter(item=>item.status!=='done').length;return {label:photosLeft?`남은 필수 사진 ${photosLeft}장`:'현장 완료',action:'field-complete-v2',disabled:!!photosLeft||req.failed||isLocked()}; }
        if(job==='upload'&&req.failed) return {label:'미전송 재시도',action:'retry-all-task-photos',disabled:isLocked()};
        if(job==='upload') return {label:'청소 전체 제출',action:'submit-cleaning-v2',disabled:!req.requiredDone||isLocked()};
        if(job==='inspection'||job==='approved') return {label:'제출 결과 보기',action:'demo-info',disabled:false};
        return {label:'상세 확인',action:'demo-info',disabled:false};
      }
      function maidCanStart(no) {
        const room=ROOMS.find(r=>r.no===no),job=state.jobs[no],task=taskState(no),attemptId=currentAttemptId(no),attempt=state.cleaningAttempts?.[attemptId];
        return state.role==='maid'&&!isLocked()&&(signedInMaidIsActive()||maidCanCompleteRequiredReclean(no))&&room?.assignee===signedInMaidName()&&task.attemptId===attemptId&&attempt?.performerId===signedInMaidId()&&['active','reclean'].includes(attempt.status)&&['scheduled','claimed','reclean'].includes(job)&&attemptAccessStatus(no,attempt).allowed;
      }
      function maidCanEditCleaning(no,allowedJobs=['cleaning']) {
        const room=ROOMS.find(item=>item.no===no),task=taskState(no),attempt=state.cleaningAttempts?.[task.attemptId];

      }
