        const candleBody=`<p class="audit-note" style="margin:12px 0 0">청소 결과를 입력하는 동안만 객실의 최종 촛불 수량을 조정할 수 있습니다.</p><div class="candle-stepper" role="group" aria-label="${no}호 청소 결과 촛불 수량"><button class="btn btn-outline" type="button" data-action="task-candle-change" data-room="${no}" data-delta="-1" aria-label="촛불 1개 줄이기" ${!editable||task.candle<1?'disabled':''}>−</button><div class="candle-stepper-value" aria-live="polite"><strong>${task.candle||0}개</strong><span>청소 완료 후 객실 수량</span></div><button class="btn btn-outline" type="button" data-action="task-candle-change" data-room="${no}" data-delta="1" aria-label="촛불 1개 늘리기" ${!editable||task.candle>=9?'disabled':''}>+</button></div>`;
        return `<div class="cleaning-sections">${renderCleaningInputSection(no,'photos','구역별 사진 촬영',`${completedGroups}/${requiredGroups.length} 구역`,photoBody)}${renderCleaningInputSection(no,'bomb-room','폭탄방 신고',bombMeta.label,renderBombRoomPanel(no,job))}${renderCleaningInputSection(no,'room-issues','객실 특이사항',`${visibleRoomIssueRecords(no).length}건 · 선택 ${task.issueDraft.photos.length}장`,renderRoomIssueInput(no,editable))}${occupied?'':renderCleaningInputSection(no,'candles','촛불',`${task.candle||0}개`,candleBody)}</div>`;
      }
      function cleaningPrimary(no,job) {
        const req=taskRequirements(no),access=attemptAccessStatus(no);
        if(['scheduled','claimed','reclean'].includes(job)) return {label:access.allowed?(job==='reclean'?'재청소 시작':'일 시작'):access.reason,action:'start-cleaning',disabled:!access.allowed||isLocked()};
        if(job==='cleaning') { const photosLeft=req.requiredUploads.filter(item=>item.status!=='done').length;return {label:photosLeft?`남은 필수 사진 ${photosLeft}장`:'현장 완료',action:'field-complete-v2',disabled:!!photosLeft||req.failed||isLocked()}; }
        if(job==='upload'&&req.failed) return {label:'미전송 재시도',action:'retry-all-task-photos',disabled:isLocked()};
        if(job==='upload') return {label:'청소 전체 제출',action:'submit-cleaning-v2',disabled:!req.requiredDone||isLocked()};
        if(job==='inspection'||job==='approved') return {label:'제출 결과 보기',action:'demo-info',disabled:false};
        return {label:'상세 확인',action:'demo-info',disabled:false};
      }
      function maidCanStart(no) {
        const room=ROOMS.find(r=>r.no===no),job=state.jobs[no],task=taskState(no),attemptId=currentAttemptId(no),attempt=state.cleaningAttempts?.[attemptId];
        return state.role==='maid'&&!isLocked()&&(signedInMaidIsActive()||maidCanCompleteRequiredReclean(no))&&room?.assignee===signedInMaidName()&&task.attemptId===attemptId&&attempt?.performerId===signedInMaidId()&&['active','reclean'].includes(attempt.status)&&['scheduled','claimed','reclean'].includes(job)&&attemptAccessStatus(no,attempt).allowed;
      }
      function maidCanEditCleaning(no,allowedJobs=['cleaning']) {
        const room=ROOMS.find(item=>item.no===no),task=taskState(no),attempt=state.cleaningAttempts?.[task.attemptId];
        return state.role==='maid'&&!isLocked()&&(signedInMaidIsActive()||maidCanContinueDeactivation(no)||maidCanCompleteRequiredReclean(no))&&room?.assignee===signedInMaidName()&&task.attemptId===currentAttemptId(no)&&attempt?.performerId===signedInMaidId()&&allowedJobs.includes(state.jobs[no]);
      }
      function renderCleaningDetailActiveCore(no) {
        const baseRoom=ROOMS.find(r=>r.no===no)||ROOMS[0],currentAttempt=state.cleaningAttempts?.[currentAttemptId(no)]||null,currentRecord=currentSubmission(no);
        if(!state.jobs[no]&&!currentAttempt&&!currentRecord)return state.role==='admin'?renderRoomDetail(no):renderCoach()+detailHeader('청소 작업이 없습니다','관리자에게 통보받은 본인 업무만 확인할 수 있습니다.')+'<section class="card card-pad"><div class="notice notice-info"><div><strong>생성된 청소 작업이 없습니다.</strong><br>객실 기본정보를 청소 배정으로 간주하지 않습니다.</div></div></section>';
        const room=no==='332'&&state.conflict==='resolved'?{...baseRoom,checkout:state.conflictRecord.afterCheckout}:baseRoom,job=state.jobs[no];
        const activeAttempt=state.cleaningAttempts?.[currentAttemptId(no)],activeGuestCount=guestCountForAttempt(activeAttempt),activeCheckout=activeAttempt?.checkoutSnapshot||room.checkout,activeCheckin=activeAttempt?.checkinSnapshot||room.checkin,activeRoomMeta=activeAttempt?.roomMetaSnapshot||roomMetadataSnapshot(no),activeType=ROOM_TYPES[activeRoomMeta?.typeId]||ROOM_TYPES[room.type],activeTypeName=activeRoomMeta?.typeName||activeType.name,activeTemplate=snapshotForAttempt(no,activeAttempt),activeRate=activeAttempt?.baseRateSnapshot??activeType.rate,activeKind=activeAttempt?.kind||(job==='reclean'?'재청소':'퇴실 청소'),activeMinutes=activeTemplate?.minutes??activeType.minutes,activeStageLabel=activeKind==='재청소'?(job==='reclean'?'재청소 · 시작 예정':`재청소 · ${statusLabel(job)}`):cleaningLabel(job);
        const maidOwnsSubmission=['inspection','approved'].includes(job)&&currentSubmission(no)?.performerId===signedInMaidId();
        if(state.role==='maid'&&room.assignee!==signedInMaidName()&&!maidOwnsSubmission)return renderCoach()+detailHeader('접근할 수 없는 업무','본인에게 배정되었거나 본인이 제출한 객실만 확인할 수 있습니다.')+'<section class="card card-pad"><div class="notice notice-danger"><div><strong>업무 접근이 차단됐습니다.</strong><br>목록으로 돌아가 관리자에게 배정받은 업무를 확인해 주세요.</div></div></section>';
        if(job==='approved'&&!currentSubmission(no))return renderInspectionDetail(no);
        if(state.role==='admin'&&(['inspection','approved'].includes(job)||(state.inspections[no]==='rejected'&&state.detail?.mode!=='reclean'))) return renderInspectionDetail(no);
        const access=attemptAccessStatus(no,activeAttempt),rollover=rolloverMetaForRoom(no),start=access.start, reached=access.allowed, primary=cleaningPrimary(no,job), occupied=room.occupancy==='occupied', special=roomReservationStatus(room), preparationDeadline=activeAttempt?.deadlineSnapshot||shiftClockTime(special.checkin,-30)||'마감 없음';
        if(state.role==='admin') return renderCoach()+renderNetworkNotice()+detailHeader(`${no}호 · ${activeStageLabel}`,`${activeKind} · ${activeTypeName} · ${money(activeRate)} · ${activeMinutes}분 · ${activeTemplate?.version||'데모'}`)+`<div class="detail-grid"><div class="detail-stack"><section class="card card-pad"><div class="section-head"><h3>작업 상태</h3>${statusBadge(statusLabel(job),['cleaning','upload'].includes(job)?'amber':'neutral')}</div>${rolloverBadgeMarkup(rollover)}<div class="info-grid"><div class="info-item"><span>담당</span><strong>${esc(room.assignee)}</strong></div><div class="info-item"><span>시작 가능</span><strong>${start}</strong></div>${rollover?`<div class="info-item"><span>계획 서비스일</span><strong>${esc(dateLabel(rollover.planDate))}</strong></div><div class="info-item"><span>현재 수행일</span><strong>${esc(dateLabel(rollover.effectiveDate))}</strong></div>`:''}<div class="info-item"><span>숙박 인원</span><strong>${activeGuestCount?guestCountLabel(activeGuestCount):'미기록'}</strong></div><div class="info-item"><span>이전 예약 체크아웃 → 다음 예약 체크인</span><strong>${esc(activeCheckout)} → ${esc(activeCheckin)}</strong></div><div class="info-item"><span>현재 단계</span><strong>${esc(activeStageLabel)}</strong></div></div>${activeKind==='재청소'?`<div class="notice notice-info" style="margin-top:12px"><div><strong>처음 청소한 본인에게 고정된 무급 재청소입니다.</strong><br>담당 취소·이관·별도 보상·폭탄방 추가요금 없이 완료 이력만 남깁니다.</div></div>`:''}${['public','draft','future','scheduled'].includes(job)?`<div style="margin-top:12px">${button('메이드 직접 배정','direct-assign','primary',`data-id="${no}"`)}</div>`:''}</section>${job==='upload'?`<section class="card card-pad">${renderTaskInputSections(no,job)}</section>`:''}</div><aside class="detail-stack"><section class="card card-pad"><h3>수행·제출 타임라인</h3>${renderCleaningTimeline(no,job)}</section></aside></div>`;
        const activeCleaning=activeCleaningFor();
        return renderCoach()+renderNetworkNotice()+detailHeader(`${no}호 · ${activeStageLabel}`,`${activeKind} · ${activeTypeName} · ${money(activeRate)} · ${activeMinutes}분 · ${activeTemplate?.version||'데모'}`)+`<div class="detail-grid"><div class="detail-stack"><section class="card card-pad"><div class="section-head"><h3>담당·출입</h3>${statusBadge(statusLabel(job),job==='cleaning'||job==='upload'?'amber':'neutral')}</div>${rolloverBadgeMarkup(rollover)}<div class="info-grid"><div class="info-item"><span>책임 메이드</span><strong>${esc(room.assignee)}</strong></div><div class="info-item"><span>업무일·출입</span><strong>${esc(access.effectiveDate||access.workDate||'확인 필요')} · ${esc(start)}${access.end?`–${esc(access.end)}`:''} · ${esc(access.reason)}</strong></div>${rollover?`<div class="info-item"><span>계획 서비스일</span><strong>${esc(dateLabel(rollover.planDate))}</strong></div><div class="info-item"><span>현재 수행일</span><strong>${esc(dateLabel(rollover.effectiveDate))}</strong></div>`:''}<div class="info-item"><span>숙박 인원</span><strong>${activeGuestCount?guestCountLabel(activeGuestCount):'미기록'}</strong></div><div class="info-item"><span>체크아웃</span><strong>${esc(activeCheckout)}</strong></div><div class="info-item"><span>준비 마감</span><strong>${preparationDeadline}</strong></div>${special.early?`<div class="info-item"><span>자동 체크인 상태</span><strong>${esc(reservationStatusText(special,'checkin'))}</strong></div>`:''}${special.late?`<div class="info-item"><span>자동 체크아웃 상태</span><strong>${esc(reservationStatusText(special,'checkout'))}</strong></div>`:''}</div>${['claimed','scheduled','reclean','cleaning'].includes(job)?`<div style="margin-top:12px">${renderPinRow(no,{editable:false,maid:true})}</div>`:''}${activeCleaning&&activeCleaning!==no&&['claimed','reclean'].includes(job)?`<div class="notice notice-warning" style="margin-top:12px">${activeCleaning}호가 이미 청소 중입니다. 현장 완료 전에는 이 작업을 시작할 수 없습니다.</div>`:''}</section><section class="card card-pad">${renderTaskInputSections(no,job)}</section></div><aside class="detail-stack"><section class="card card-pad"><h3>수행·제출 타임라인</h3>${renderCleaningTimeline(no,job)}</section>${activeKind==='재청소'?`<section class="card card-pad"><h3>재청소 담당 고정</h3><p class="audit-note">처음 청소한 본인에게만 귀속되는 무급 작업입니다. 담당 취소·이관·별도 보상은 지원하지 않습니다.</p>${statusBadge('본인 수행 · 0원','neutral')}</section>`:!['upload','inspection','approved'].includes(job)?`<section class="card card-pad"><h3>담당 취소</h3><p class="audit-note">현장 완료 전까지만 요청할 수 있고 관리자 승인 전 담당은 유지됩니다.</p>${button('담당 취소 요청','request-cancel','outline')}</section>`:''}</aside></div><div class="sticky-command"><button class="btn ${job==='upload'&&taskRequirements(no).failed?'btn-danger':'btn-primary'}" type="button" data-action="${primary.action}" data-id="${no}" ${primary.disabled?'disabled':''}>${primary.label}</button></div>`;
      }

      function renderCleaningDetail(no) {
        let html=renderCleaningDetailActiveCore(no);
        if(state.role!=='admin'||state.jobs[no]==='upload')return html;
        if(state.jobs[no]==='inspection')html=html.replace('<strong>특이사항</strong>','<strong>기본 제출 메모</strong>');
        const stackEnd='</div><aside class="detail-stack">',insertAt=html.indexOf(stackEnd);
        if(insertAt<0)return html;
        const submittedContext=['inspection','approved'].includes(state.jobs[no])||state.inspections[no]==='rejected',submission=submittedContext?(currentSubmission(no)||currentSubmissionRecord(no)):null,submittedIssues=submittedContext?(submission?.roomIssuesSnapshot||[]):undefined;
        return html.slice(0,insertAt)+renderRoomIssuesSection(no,submittedIssues)+html.slice(insertAt);
      }

      function renderInspectionDetail(no) {
        const room=ROOMS.find(r=>r.no===no)||ROOMS[0],status=state.inspections[no]||'pending',validSubmission=currentSubmission(no),submission=validSubmission||currentSubmissionRecord(no),submissionIntegrityOk=!!validSubmission,attempt=submission?.attemptId?state.cleaningAttempts?.[submission.attemptId]:null,submissionRoomMeta=submission?.roomMetaSnapshot||attempt?.roomMetaSnapshot||roomMetadataSnapshot(no),type=ROOM_TYPES[submissionRoomMeta?.typeId]||ROOM_TYPES[room.type],submittedTypeName=submissionRoomMeta?.typeName||type.name,submissionTemplate=templateSnapshotForSubmission(no,submission,attempt),report=submissionIntegrityOk?submittedBombRoomReport(no):rawBombRoomReportForSubmission(submission),bombPending=report?.status==='pending',performer=submission?.performerName||room.assignee,startedAt=String(attempt?.startedAt||'—').split(' ').pop(),completedAt=String(submission?.completedAt||attempt?.completedAt||'—').split(' ').pop(),submissionNote=submission?.note?.trim()||'등록된 기본 제출 메모 없음';
        if(!submission&&state.jobs[no]==='approved')return renderCoach()+renderNetworkNotice()+detailHeader(`${no}호 청소 완료 결과`,`과거 완료 데모 스냅샷 · 주급 원장 비연결 · 읽기 전용`)+`<div class="detail-grid"><div class="detail-stack"><section class="card card-pad"><div class="section-head"><h3>전체 검수 결과</h3>${statusBadge('승인 완료','green')}</div><div class="info-grid"><div class="info-item"><span>객실·타입</span><strong>${no}호 · ${esc(type.name)}</strong></div><div class="info-item"><span>실제 수행자</span><strong>상세 제출 원장 없음</strong></div><div class="info-item"><span>객실 타입 참고요금</span><strong>${money(type.rate)} · 정산값 아님</strong></div><div class="info-item"><span>객실 결과</span><strong>준비 완료 · 촛불 ${state.candles[no]||0}개</strong></div></div><div class="notice notice-success" style="margin-top:14px"><div><strong>완료 상태만 보존한 과거 데모 기록입니다.</strong><br>검수 대기 액션은 제공하지 않으며, 실제 수행자·제출 사진·정산 금액은 이 스냅샷으로 확정하지 않습니다.</div></div></section></div><aside class="detail-stack"><section class="card card-pad"><h3>데이터 범위</h3><p class="audit-note">실제 서버 제출이나 주급 원장과 연결되지 않은 화면 검증용 fixture입니다. 주급 화면의 사람별 데모 원장을 별도로 확인하세요.</p></section></aside></div>`;
        const earning=earningBreakdown(no),decisionLocked=isLocked()||bombPending||!submissionIntegrityOk,unpaidReclean=submission?.kind==='재청소',submittedChecks=submissionTemplate?.checklist||[],completedChecks=submittedChecks.filter(item=>submission?.checklist?.[item.id]),requiredUploads=(submissionTemplate?.photos||[]).filter(item=>item.required),submittedUploads=submission?.uploads||[],submittedPhotosDone=submittedUploads.filter(item=>item.status==='done').length,submittedIssues=submission?.roomIssuesSnapshot||[],submittedIssuePhotos=submittedIssues.reduce((total,item)=>total+(item.photos?.length||0),0),submittedCandle=submission?.candleCountSnapshot??submission?.candle??0;

      function openMaidDeactivationV2(maidId,trigger=document.activeElement) {

        if(!maid)return;

          const requestedAt=`${state.selectedDate} ${state.time}`,planDate=attemptWorkDate(attempt,state.selectedDate),workDate=attemptOperationalDate(attempt,state.selectedDate);
          state.cancelRequests[no]={status:'requested',reason,requestedAt,planDate,workDate,workTargetId:attempt.workTargetId||assignmentHistoryTargetId(no,attempt.kind,planDate),maid:room.assignee,performerId:attempt.performerId,job:jobBefore,attemptId:attempt.id};state.cancelRequest='requested';state.cancelRequestRoom=no;
          appendEvent(`${no}호 담당 취소 요청`,`${room.assignee} · ${attempt.id} · ${reason} · 관리자 결정 전 담당 유지`,{maidIds:[attempt.performerId]});
          closeModal();render();toast('관리자 확인 요청을 보냈습니다. 현재 담당은 유지됩니다.');return;
        }
        if(a==='cancel-review'){
          if(!adminCanMutate()){toast('관리자 최신 상태에서만 담당 취소 요청을 처리할 수 있습니다.','error');return;}
          const requested=Object.values(state.cancelRequests||{}).some(request=>request.status==='requested')||state.cancelRequest==='requested';
          if(!requested){toast('담당 취소 요청은 이미 처리됐습니다.');return;}

        }
        if(a==='confirm-cancel'){

          const chosen=document.querySelector('input[name="cancel-decision"]:checked')?.value||'deny',no=state.cancelRequestRoom||Object.keys(state.cancelRequests||{})[0]||'332',request=state.cancelRequests?.[no],room=ROOMS.find(item=>item.no===no),maid=request?.maid||room?.assignee||'김민지1',attemptId=state.currentAttemptByRoom?.[no],previousAttempt=attemptId?state.cleaningAttempts?.[attemptId]:null,allowedJobs=['claimed','scheduled','cleaning'];

          if(chosen==='direct'&&request.performerId==='m2'){toast('현재 담당과 같은 김민지2에게 다시 배정할 수 없습니다. 다른 결정을 선택하세요.','error');return;}
          if(chosen==='direct'&&availabilityForWorkDate('m2',request.workDate)!=='available'){toast('김민지2가 요청 업무 주차의 해당 날짜를 근무 가능으로 제출하지 않아 직접 배정할 수 없습니다.','error');return;}

          const attemptOptions={reason:'담당 취소 승인 뒤 새 책임 구간',baseRateSnapshot:previousAttempt.baseRateSnapshot,kind:previousAttempt.kind||'퇴실 청소',templateSnapshot:previousAttempt.templateSnapshot||templateSnapshotFor(no,previousAttempt.kind),accessStart:previousAttempt.accessStart,requestDue:previousAttempt.requestDue,accessEnd:previousAttempt.accessEnd,reservationIdSnapshot:previousAttempt.reservationIdSnapshot,guestCountSnapshot:previousAttempt.guestCountSnapshot,checkoutSnapshot:previousAttempt.checkoutSnapshot,checkinSnapshot:previousAttempt.checkinSnapshot,deadlineSnapshot:previousAttempt.deadlineSnapshot,nextReservationIdSnapshot:previousAttempt.nextReservationIdSnapshot};
          state.cancelDecision=chosen;state.cancelRequest='resolved';if(request){request.status=chosen==='deny'?'denied':'approved';request.decision=chosen;request.decidedAt=`${state.selectedDate} ${state.time}`;}
          let replacementAttempt=null;
          if(chosen==='republish'){room.assignee='미정';replacementAttempt=beginCleaningAttempt(no,{...attemptOptions,performerName:'미정',workDate:request.planDate||request.workDate,effectiveDate:request.workDate,workTargetId:requestTargetId});state.jobs[no]='unassigned';}
          if(chosen==='direct'){room.assignee='김민지2';replacementAttempt=beginCleaningAttempt(no,{...attemptOptions,performerName:'김민지2',workDate:request.planDate||request.workDate,effectiveDate:request.workDate,workTargetId:requestTargetId});state.jobs[no]='claimed';}
          if(chosen==='hold'){room.assignee='미정';replacementAttempt=beginCleaningAttempt(no,{...attemptOptions,performerName:'미정',workDate:request.planDate||request.workDate,effectiveDate:request.workDate,workTargetId:requestTargetId});state.jobs[no]='hold';}
